 # Arquitetura AS-IS
 
-Base inicial: bootstrap `mcp-orcamento` (`0.1.0`, `ADR-0001`).
+Base atual: `TCK-0001` (`v0.1.0`, `ADR-0001`, implementado).
 
 Arquitetura dual-runtime herdada do molde `mcp-dadosbr`:
-- **Node.js CLI (`lib/bin/mcp-orcamento.ts` -> `lib/adapters/cli.ts`, `cli-http.ts`):** transportes `stdio` e `HTTP/SSE`, métricas e logs JSON exclusivamente em `stderr`, `MemoryCache`, `RateLimiter` e `createGracefulShutdown`.
+- **Node.js CLI (`lib/bin/mcp-orcamento.ts` -> `lib/adapters/cli.ts`, `cli-http.ts`):** transportes `stdio` e `HTTP/SSE` + `StreamableHTTPServerTransport`, métricas `[metrics]` e logs JSON exclusivamente em `stderr`, `MemoryCache`, `RateLimiter` e encerramento gracioso (`shutdownStdio` / `RunningHttpServer.close()`).
 - **Cloudflare Workers (`lib/adapters/cloudflare.ts` -> `lib/workers/*`):** runtime serverless *stateless* sem imports `node:*` na camada core/shared/tools/workers, cache/rate-limit em KV, REST genérico `POST /tools/{toolName}` e OpenAPI 3.0.
 - **Camada acíclica (`lib/tools/` -> `lib/core/` -> `lib/shared/`):** `lib/core/` não importa `lib/tools/`.

 # Segurança e limites
 
-Base inicial: bootstrap `mcp-orcamento` (`0.1.0`, `SPC-0001`).
+Base atual: `TCK-0001` (`v0.1.0`, `SPC-0001`, implementado).
 
-- **SSRF:** allowlists HTTPS explícitas para Tesouro ORDS, Transferegov e Tesouro CKAN; loopback apenas em `NODE_ENV=development|test`, rejeição de credenciais embutidas na URL e validação de `.mcprc.json` e overrides.
+- **SSRF:** allowlists HTTPS explícitas para Tesouro ORDS, Transferegov e Tesouro CKAN (`lib/shared/utils/constants.ts`, re-exportadas por `lib/config/index.ts`); loopback apenas em `NODE_ENV=development|test` (ou `allowLoopback: true`), rejeição de credenciais embutidas na URL e validação de `.mcprc.json` e overrides.
 - **Anti-injeção PostgREST:** filtros textuais (`nome_parlamentar`, `sigla_unidade`) removem `[,()*%\\]` antes de montar `ilike.*clean*`.
 - **Validação agêntica (`SC-9`):** todas as 8 ferramentas usam Zod `.strict()` sem `api_key`, allowlist versionada em `.archagents/mcp-tool-allowlist.json` (`_capabilities.json` com `enabled: false`, `tool_count: 8`), sanitização `sanitizeUntrustedText`, aviso `UNTRUSTED_EXTERNAL_DATA_NOTICE`, teto `serializeBoundedToolOutput` e `lgpd_notice` (`HOMONYM_HUMAN_REVIEW_NOTICE`) em resultados com nomes parlamentares.
-- **Auth & CORS:** `MCP_API_KEY` fail-closed fora de loopback no CLI HTTP e em rotas privadas do Worker; OAuth desabilitado fail-closed (`501`).
+- **Auth, DNS Rebinding & CORS:** `cli-http.ts` faz bind padrão em `127.0.0.1`, exige `MCP_API_KEY` fail-closed fora de loopback (com comparação `SHA-256` + `timingSafeEqual`), valida sintaxe de porta em `Host` e `Origin` estrito; no Worker (`lib/workers/auth.ts`), `MCP_REQUIRE_AUTH=true` opera fail-closed (`401`) se nenhuma chave estiver provisionada e OAuth não configurado retorna `501`.

 ## Observabilidade e Logs
 
 - **Logs JSON em `stderr` (`lib/infrastructure/logger.ts`):** todos os níveis escrevem exclusivamente via `console.error`, mantendo `stdout` reservado para JSON-RPC no transporte `stdio`.
-- **Correlação `X-Request-Id` (`lib/infrastructure/tracing.ts`):** Web Crypto (`globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()` / `getRandomValues()`), propagação por `AsyncLocalStorage` no CLI HTTP e por `RequestContext` no Worker.
-- **Health check (`GET /health`):** sonda opcional com timeout de `1000ms` sobre `MCP_CACHE` / `MCP_KV`, retornando `200` (`healthy`) ou `503` (`degraded`).
+- **Correlação `X-Request-Id` (`lib/infrastructure/tracing.ts`):** Web Crypto (`globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()` / `getRandomValues()`), propagação por `res.locals.requestContext` no CLI HTTP (`lib/adapters/cli-http.ts`) e `RequestContext` explícito no Worker (`lib/workers/worker.ts`).
+- **Health check (`GET /health`):** retorna `200` (`healthy`) com `service`, `version`, `tools` (`8`), indicação `kv_bound` (no Worker), `privacy` (`dpo_contact`, `lgpd_notice`) e métricas RED (`requests`, `cacheHits`, `errors`, `uptimeMs`).

 # MCP Orçamento (`@aredes.me/mcp-orcamento`)
 
-Servidor Model Context Protocol (MCP) em TypeScript (`v0.1.0`, `AGPL-3.0-only`) para consultas read-only e sem API key às bases públicas brasileiras de orçamento, execução fiscal, transferências e operações de crédito (SICONFI, Transferegov, SADIPEM/PVL e Tesouro Transparente CKAN).
+Servidor [Model Context Protocol (MCP)](https://modelcontextprotocol.io/) em TypeScript (`v0.1.0`, `AGPL-3.0-only`) para consultas **somente leitura** e **sem necessidade de API key** às principais APIs públicas brasileiras de orçamento, execução fiscal, transferências federais e operações de crédito:
 
-> Bootstrap inicial — consulte [`.archagents/README.md`](.archagents/README.md).
+- **SICONFI (Tesouro Nacional ORDS):** entes federativos, extrato de entregas, RREO, RGF e DCA
+- **Transferegov (MGI PostgREST):** Transferências Especiais (*Emendas Pix* / art. 166-A), Fundo a Fundo e Termos de Execução Descentralizada (TED)
+- **SADIPEM / PVL & Tesouro Transparente CKAN:** pedidos de verificação de limites e condições (operações de crédito subnacionais) e busca de datasets abertos
+
+> Documentação canônica e rastreabilidade `codebase-ops`: [`.archagents/README.md`](.archagents/README.md) · Privacidade/LGPD: [`PRIVACY.md`](PRIVACY.md) · Licenciamento/Atribuições: [`NOTICE.md`](NOTICE.md)
+
+## Catálogo de Ferramentas (8 tools)
+
+| Ferramenta | Fonte | Descrição |
+|---|---|---|
+| `siconfi_entes` | STN SICONFI ORDS | Lista e filtra entes federativos (IBGE, CNPJ, UF, esfera `U/E/M/D`, capitais, nome) |
+| `siconfi_extrato_entregas` | STN SICONFI ORDS | Histórico de entregas de demonstrativos (`RREO`, `RGF`, `DCA`, `MSC`) por ente e ano |
+| `siconfi_rreo` | STN SICONFI ORDS | Relatório Resumido da Execução Orçamentária com `anexos_disponiveis` e paginação |
+| `siconfi_rgf` | STN SICONFI ORDS | Relatório de Gestão Fiscal por periodicidade (`Q`/`S`), poder (`E/L/J/M/D`) e anexo |
+| `siconfi_dca` | STN SICONFI ORDS | Declaração de Contas Anuais com descoberta de anexos e filtro local por conta |
+| `transferegov_emendas_especiais` | Transferegov PostgREST | Planos de ação, executores, empenhos, ordens de pagamento/bancárias e relatórios |
+| `transferegov_fundo_a_fundo_ted` | Transferegov PostgREST | Recursos de transferências Fundo a Fundo (`fundoafundo/*`) e TED (`ted/*`) |
+| `tesouro_operacoes_credito_ckan` | STN SADIPEM & CKAN | Operações de crédito subnacionais (`operacoes_credito_pvl`) e datasets (`ckan_datasets`) |
+
+## Uso Rápido
+
+```bash
+npm install
+npm run build
+npm run build:worker
+
+# Transporte stdio (padrão para hosts MCP)
+npm start
+
+# Transporte HTTP + SSE local endurecido (127.0.0.1:3000)
+node build/lib/bin/mcp-orcamento.js --http --port 3000
+```
+
+### Variáveis de Ambiente
+
+| Variável | Padrão | Descrição |
+|---|---|---|
+| `MCP_TRANSPORT` | `stdio` | `stdio` ou `http` |
+| `MCP_HTTP_HOST` / `MCP_HTTP_PORT` | `127.0.0.1` / `3000` | Bind do servidor HTTP Node |
+| `MCP_API_KEY` / `API_KEY` | *(opcional)* | Habilita autenticação `Bearer` / `X-API-Key` nas rotas HTTP/Worker protegidas |
+| `MCP_ORDS_TIMEOUT` | `15000` | Timeout (ms) para chamadas ORDS (SICONFI / SADIPEM) |
+| `MCP_HTTP_TIMEOUT` | `8000` | Timeout (ms) para chamadas HTTP padrão (Transferegov / CKAN) |
+| `MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000` | Limite de tamanho do JSON retornado por tool |
+| `TESOURO_ORDS_BASE_URL` | `https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/` | Override allowlisted da API Tesouro ORDS |
+| `TRANSFEREGOV_BASE_URL` | `https://api.transferegov.gestao.gov.br/` | Override allowlisted da API Transferegov |
+| `TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL` | `https://www.tesourotransparente.gov.br/ckan/api/3/action/` | Override allowlisted da API CKAN |
+| `MCP_DPO_CONTACT` | *(opcional)* | Contato de DPO exposto em `GET /health` no Cloudflare Worker |
+
+### Cloudflare Worker
+
+O worker em [`lib/workers/worker.ts`](lib/workers/worker.ts) expõe:
+- `GET /` — manifesto do serviço e lista das 8 ferramentas
+- `GET /health` — status, métricas RED e informações de privacidade (`privacy.dpo_contact`)
+- `GET /openapi.json` — especificação OpenAPI 3.0.3
+- `POST /mcp` — endpoint JSON-RPC 2.0 MCP
+- `GET|POST /sse` — transporte Server-Sent Events com persistência opcional em KV
+- `POST /tools/{toolName}` — endpoint REST genérico validado pela allowlist de 8 ferramentas
+
+## Segurança e Conformidade
+
+- **Proteção SSRF (`lib/shared/utils/constants.ts`, `lib/config/index.ts`):** HTTPS obrigatório fora de `development`/`test`, bloqueio de IPs privados/metadados e credenciais embutidas na URL, e allowlists explícitas por domínio oficial.
+- **Anti-injeção PostgREST (`lib/shared/orcamento-schemas.ts`, `lib/tools/transferegov.ts`):** remoção de operadores `[,()*%\\]` antes de construir expressões `ilike.*clean*`.
+- **Controles Agênticos & LGPD (`lib/shared/agentic-guard.ts`, `lib/shared/compliance-notices.ts`):** validação Zod `.strict()`, sanitização de strings externas, `untrusted_data_notice`, truncamento seguro e `lgpd_notice` (`HOMONYM_HUMAN_REVIEW_NOTICE`) quando há nomes parlamentares/nominais.
+
+## Desenvolvimento e Testes
+
+```bash
+npx tsc --noEmit
+npx tsc -p tsconfig.worker.json --noEmit
+npm run test:coverage
+npm run test:transport
+```
diff --git a/lib/adapters/cli-http.ts b/lib/adapters/cli-http.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..33d486c

+++ b/lib/adapters/cli-http.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,512 @@
+/**
+ * Hardened Node Express 5 HTTP/SSE & Streamable HTTP MCP transport adapter.
+ * Features loopback default binding, DNS rebinding Host validation, strict CORS,
+ * timing-safe API key auth, security headers, 100KB body limit, and sliding-window rate limiting.
+ */
+
+import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
+import http from "node:http";
+import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
+import { SSEServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/sse.js";
+import { StreamableHTTPServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/streamableHttp.js";
+import express, {
+  type Express,
+  type NextFunction,
+  type Request,
+  type Response,
+} from "express";
+import { handleMCPRequest } from "./cloudflare.js";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../core/cache.js";
+import { createMCPServer } from "../core/mcp-server.js";
+import { getMetrics, TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "../core/tools.js";
+import { logger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import { RateLimiter } from "../infrastructure/rate-limiter.js";
+import {
+  createRequestContext,
+  generateRequestId,
+  REQUEST_ID_HEADER,
+} from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { SERVER } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import type { ApiConfig, Cache, MCPRequest, ServerConfig } from "../types/index.js";
+
+const LOOPBACK_HOSTS = new Set(["127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1", "[::1]"]);
+
+export function isLoopbackHost(host: string): boolean {
+  if (typeof host !== "string") return false;
+  return LOOPBACK_HOSTS.has(host.trim().toLowerCase());
+}
+
+export function isValidApiKey(provided: string, expected: string): boolean {
+  if (!provided || !expected) return false;
+  const providedDigest = createHash("sha256").update(provided, "utf8").digest();
+  const expectedDigest = createHash("sha256").update(expected, "utf8").digest();
+  return timingSafeEqual(providedDigest, expectedDigest);
+}
+
+export interface HttpServerOptions {
+  apiConfig: ApiConfig;
+  serverConfig: ServerConfig;
+  cache?: Cache;
+  apiKey?: string;
+  allowedOrigins?: string[];
+  allowedHosts?: string[];
+  rateLimitMax?: number;
+}
+
+export interface RunningHttpServer {
+  app: Express;
+  server: http.Server;
+  port: number;
+  host: string;
+  close: () => Promise<void>;
+}
+
+function normalizeHostHeader(rawHost: string): string | null {
+  const trimmed = rawHost.trim().toLowerCase();
+  if (!trimmed || /[\s/@\\?#]/.test(trimmed)) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  if (trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
+    const match = /^\[([a-f0-9:.]+)\](?::(\d{1,5}))?$/.exec(trimmed);
+    if (!match) return null;
+    if (match[2] !== undefined) {
+      const port = Number(match[2]);
+      if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) return null;
+    }
+    return `[${match[1]}]`;
+  }
+  const firstColon = trimmed.indexOf(":");
+  if (firstColon === -1) return trimmed;
+  if (firstColon !== trimmed.lastIndexOf(":")) return null;
+  const hostname = trimmed.slice(0, firstColon);
+  const portStr = trimmed.slice(firstColon + 1);
+  if (!hostname || !/^\d{1,5}$/.test(portStr)) return null;
+  const port = Number(portStr);
+  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) return null;
+  return hostname;
+}
+
+function isOriginAllowed(origin: string, explicitAllowlist?: string[]): boolean {
+  if (explicitAllowlist && explicitAllowlist.length > 0) {
+    return explicitAllowlist.includes(origin);
+  }
+  try {
+    const parsed = new URL(origin);
+    if (
+      (parsed.protocol !== "http:" && parsed.protocol !== "https:") ||
+      parsed.username ||
+      parsed.password ||
+      parsed.pathname !== "/" ||
+      parsed.search !== "" ||
+      parsed.hash !== "" ||
+      origin.endsWith("/")
+    ) {
+      return false;
+    }
+    return LOOPBACK_HOSTS.has(parsed.hostname.toLowerCase());
+  } catch {
+    return false;
+  }
+}
+
+function parseListEnv(key: string): string[] | undefined {
+  const raw = process.env[key];
+  if (!raw || raw.trim().length === 0) return undefined;
+  return raw
+    .split(",")
+    .map((s) => s.trim())
+    .filter((s) => s.length > 0);
+}
+
+function extractBearerOrApiKey(req: Request): string | undefined {
+  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
+  if (typeof authHeader === "string") {
+    const match = /^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i.exec(authHeader.trim());
+    if (match && match[1].trim().length > 0) {
+      return match[1].trim();
+    }
+  }
+  const apiKey = req.headers["x-api-key"];
+  if (typeof apiKey === "string" && apiKey.trim().length > 0) {
+    return apiKey.trim();
+  }
+  return undefined;
+}
+
+export function createHttpApp(options: HttpServerOptions): {
+  app: Express;
+  cleanup: () => Promise<void>;
+} {
+  const app = express();
+  app.disable("x-powered-by");
+
+  const cache =
+    options.cache ??
+    new MemoryCache(options.serverConfig.cacheSize, options.serverConfig.cacheTTL);
+  const ownsCache = !options.cache;
+
+  const rateLimiter = new RateLimiter({
+    maxRequests:
+      options.rateLimitMax ??
+      (process.env.MCP_RATE_LIMIT ? Number(process.env.MCP_RATE_LIMIT) || 100 : 100),
+    windowMs: 60_000,
+    cleanupIntervalMs: 60_000,
+  });
+
+  const configuredApiKey =
+    options.apiKey ?? process.env.MCP_API_KEY ?? process.env.API_KEY;
+  const bindHost = (options.serverConfig.httpHost || "127.0.0.1").trim();
+  if (!isLoopbackHost(bindHost) && (!configuredApiKey || configuredApiKey.trim().length === 0)) {
+    throw new Error(
+      `Refusing to bind HTTP MCP server to non-loopback host "${bindHost}" without MCP_API_KEY`
+    );
+  }
+  const explicitAllowedHosts = options.allowedHosts ?? parseListEnv("MCP_ALLOWED_HOSTS");
+  const explicitAllowedOrigins =
+    options.allowedOrigins ??
+    parseListEnv("MCP_CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS") ??
+    parseListEnv("CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS");
+
+  const sseTransports = new Map<
+    string,
+    { transport: SSEServerTransport; server: ReturnType<typeof createMCPServer> }
+  >();
+  const streamableTransports = new Map<
+    string,
+    { transport: StreamableHTTPServerTransport; server: ReturnType<typeof createMCPServer> }
+  >();
+
+  // Request tracing & baseline security headers
+  app.use((req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
+    const reqCtx = createRequestContext({
+      headers: req.headers as Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>,
+      transport: "node-http",
+      clientIp: req.ip,
+    });
+    res.locals.requestContext = reqCtx;
+    res.setHeader(REQUEST_ID_HEADER, reqCtx.requestId);
+    res.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
+    res.setHeader("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
+    res.setHeader("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
+    res.setHeader("Content-Security-Policy", "default-src 'none'");
+    next();
+  });
+
+  // DNS rebinding Host validation
+  app.use((req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
+    const rawHost = req.headers.host;
+    if (typeof rawHost !== "string" || rawHost.trim().length === 0) {
+      res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing Host header" });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const hostname = normalizeHostHeader(rawHost);
+    if (!hostname) {
+      res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid Host header" });
+      return;
+    }
+    if (explicitAllowedHosts && explicitAllowedHosts.length > 0) {
+      const allowedNormalized = explicitAllowedHosts
+        .map((h) => normalizeHostHeader(h))
+        .filter((h): h is string => h !== null);
+      if (!allowedNormalized.includes(hostname)) {
+        res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden Host header" });
+        return;
+      }
+      next();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (LOOPBACK_HOSTS.has(bindHost.toLowerCase()) && !LOOPBACK_HOSTS.has(hostname)) {
+      res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden Host header (DNS rebinding protection)" });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    next();
+  });
+
+  // Strict CORS validation
+  app.use((req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
+    const origin = req.headers.origin;
+    if (typeof origin === "string" && origin.trim().length > 0) {
+      if (!isOriginAllowed(origin.trim(), explicitAllowedOrigins)) {
+        res.status(403).json({ error: "CORS origin not allowed" });
+        return;
+      }
+      res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin.trim());
+      res.setHeader("Vary", "Origin");
+      res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, DELETE, OPTIONS");
+      res.setHeader(
+        "Access-Control-Allow-Headers",
+        "Content-Type, Authorization, X-API-Key, X-Request-Id, Mcp-Session-Id"
+      );
+      res.setHeader("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "Mcp-Session-Id, X-Request-Id");
+    }
+
+    if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
+      res.status(204).end();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    next();
+  });
+
+  app.use(express.json({ limit: "100kb" }));
+
+  // Malformed JSON / oversized payload error handler
+  app.use((err: unknown, _req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
+    const typedErr = err as { type?: string; status?: number };
+    if (typedErr?.type === "entity.too.large" || typedErr?.status === 413) {
+      res.status(413).json({ error: "Request body too large (max 100KB)" });
+      return;
+    }
+    if (typedErr?.type === "entity.parse.failed" || typedErr instanceof SyntaxError) {
+      res.status(400).json({ error: "Malformed JSON request body" });
+      return;
+    }
+    next(err);
+  });
+
+  // Public health endpoint
+  app.get("/health", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
+    const metrics = getMetrics();
+    res.json({
+      status: "healthy",
+      service: SERVER.NAME,
+      version: SERVER.VERSION,
+      transport: "http",
+      tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS.length,
+      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
+      metrics: {
+        requests: metrics.requests,
+        cacheHits: metrics.cacheHits,
+        errors: metrics.errors,
+        uptimeMs: metrics.uptimeMs,
+      },
+    });
+  });
+
+  // Optional API Key auth middleware for MCP/SSE endpoints
+  const requireAuth = (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
+    if (!configuredApiKey) {
+      next();
+      return;
+    }
+    const token = extractBearerOrApiKey(req);
+    if (!token) {
+      res.setHeader("WWW-Authenticate", 'Bearer realm="mcp-orcamento"');
+      res.status(401).json({ error: "Unauthorized: missing API key" });
+      return;
+    }
+    if (!configuredApiKey || !isValidApiKey(token, configuredApiKey)) {
+      res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden: invalid API key" });
+      return;
+    }
+    next();
+  };
+
+  // Per-IP sliding-window rate limiter
+  const enforceRateLimit = (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
+    const clientKey = req.ip || req.socket.remoteAddress || "local";
+    const decision = rateLimiter.check(clientKey);
+    res.setHeader("X-RateLimit-Limit", String(decision.limit));
+    res.setHeader("X-RateLimit-Remaining", String(decision.remaining));
+    res.setHeader("X-RateLimit-Reset", String(Math.ceil(decision.resetAt / 1000)));
+
+    if (!decision.allowed) {
+      res.setHeader("Retry-After", String(decision.retryAfterSeconds));
+      res.status(429).json({
+        error: "Too Many Requests",
+        retryAfterSeconds: decision.retryAfterSeconds,
+      });
+      return;
+    }
+    next();
+  };
+
+  // Streamable HTTP + stateless JSON-RPC fallback on /mcp
+  app.all("/mcp", requireAuth, enforceRateLimit, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
+    try {
+      const sessionHeader = req.headers["mcp-session-id"];
+      const sessionId = typeof sessionHeader === "string" ? sessionHeader : undefined;
+
+      if (sessionId) {
+        const entry = streamableTransports.get(sessionId);
+        if (!entry) {
+          res.status(404).json({
+            jsonrpc: "2.0",
+            id: null,
+            error: { code: -32001, message: "Unknown or expired MCP session" },
+          });
+          return;
+        }
+        await entry.transport.handleRequest(req, res, req.body);
+        return;
+      }
+
+      const accept = String(req.headers.accept ?? "").toLowerCase();
+      const bodyObj =
+        typeof req.body === "object" && req.body !== null
+          ? (req.body as { method?: unknown })
+          : undefined;
+      const isInitialize = bodyObj?.method === "initialize";
+
+      if (req.method === "POST" && (accept.includes("text/event-stream") || isInitialize)) {
+        const mcpServer = createMCPServer({
+          apiConfig: options.apiConfig,
+          cache,
+          rateLimiter,
+          rateLimitKey: req.ip || "http-session",
+          transportName: "http-streamable",
+        });
+
+        const transport = new StreamableHTTPServerTransport({
+          sessionIdGenerator: () => generateRequestId(),
+          enableJsonResponse: true,
+          onsessioninitialized: (sid) => {
+            streamableTransports.set(sid, { transport, server: mcpServer });
+          },
+          onsessionclosed: async (sid) => {
+            streamableTransports.delete(sid);
+            await mcpServer.close().catch(() => {});
+          },
+        });
+
+        transport.onclose = () => {
+          if (transport.sessionId) {
+            streamableTransports.delete(transport.sessionId);
+          }
+        };
+
+        await mcpServer.connect(transport);
+        await transport.handleRequest(req, res, req.body);
+        return;
+      }
+
+      // Stateless JSON-RPC fallback for lightweight HTTP POST /mcp clients
+      if (req.method === "POST") {
+        const reqCtx = res.locals.requestContext;
+        const rpcResp = await handleMCPRequest(
+          req.body as MCPRequest,
+          {
+            TESOURO_ORDS_BASE_URL: options.apiConfig.tesouroOrdsBaseUrl,
+            TRANSFEREGOV_BASE_URL: options.apiConfig.transferegovBaseUrl,
+            TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL: options.apiConfig.tesouroCkanBaseUrl,
+            MCP_ALLOW_LOCALHOST_APIS: "true",
+          },
+          reqCtx
+        );
+        res.status(200).json(rpcResp);
+        return;
+      }
+
+      res.status(400).json({ error: "Mcp-Session-Id header is required for GET/DELETE /mcp" });
+    } catch (error) {
+      logger.error("Error handling /mcp request", {
+        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+      });
+      if (!res.headersSent) {
+        res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });
+      }
+    }
+  });
+
+  // Legacy HTTP+SSE transport (/sse + /messages)
+  app.get("/sse", requireAuth, enforceRateLimit, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
+    const mcpServer = createMCPServer({
+      apiConfig: options.apiConfig,
+      cache,
+      rateLimiter,
+      rateLimitKey: req.ip || "sse-session",
+      transportName: "http-sse",
+    });
+
+    const transport = new SSEServerTransport("/messages", res);
+    sseTransports.set(transport.sessionId, { transport, server: mcpServer });
+
+    req.on("close", () => {
+      sseTransports.delete(transport.sessionId);
+      mcpServer.close().catch(() => {});
+    });
+
+    await mcpServer.connect(transport);
+  });
+
+  app.post("/messages", requireAuth, enforceRateLimit, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
+    const sessionId =
+      typeof req.query.sessionId === "string" ? req.query.sessionId : undefined;
+    if (!sessionId) {
+      res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing sessionId query parameter" });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const entry = sseTransports.get(sessionId);
+    if (!entry) {
+      res.status(404).json({ error: "Unknown SSE sessionId" });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    await entry.transport.handlePostMessage(req, res, req.body);
+  });
+
+  const cleanup = async () => {
+    for (const { transport, server } of sseTransports.values()) {
+      await transport.close().catch(() => {});
+      await server.close().catch(() => {});
+    }
+    sseTransports.clear();
+
+    for (const { transport, server } of streamableTransports.values()) {
+      await transport.close().catch(() => {});
+      await server.close().catch(() => {});
+    }
+    streamableTransports.clear();
+
+    rateLimiter.destroy();
+    if (ownsCache && cache instanceof MemoryCache) {
+      cache.destroy();
+    }
+  };
+
+  return { app, cleanup };
+}
+
+export async function startHttpServer(
+  options: HttpServerOptions
+): Promise<RunningHttpServer> {
+  const { app, cleanup } = createHttpApp(options);
+  const host = (options.serverConfig.httpHost || "127.0.0.1").trim();
+  const port = options.serverConfig.httpPort;
+
+  const server = http.createServer(app);
+
+  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
+    server.once("error", reject);
+    server.listen(port, host, () => {
+      server.removeListener("error", reject);
+      resolve();
+    });
+  });
+
+  const addr = server.address() as AddressInfo | null;
+  const boundPort = addr?.port ?? port;
+
+  logger.info("HTTP MCP server listening", {
+    service: SERVER.NAME,
+    version: SERVER.VERSION,
+    host,
+    port: boundPort,
+    tesouroOrdsBaseUrl: options.apiConfig.tesouroOrdsBaseUrl,
+    transferegovBaseUrl: options.apiConfig.transferegovBaseUrl,
+    tesouroCkanBaseUrl: options.apiConfig.tesouroCkanBaseUrl,
+  });
+
+  const close = async () => {
+    await cleanup();
+    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
+      server.close((err) => (err ? reject(err) : resolve()));
+    });
+  };
+
+  return { app, server, port: boundPort, host, close };
+}

+/**
+ * Node CLI entrypoint supporting stdio (default) and HTTP/SSE transports.
+ */
+
+import { realpathSync } from "node:fs";
+import path from "node:path";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
+import { StdioServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js";
+import { loadServerConfiguration } from "../config/index.js";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../core/cache.js";
+import { createMCPServer } from "../core/mcp-server.js";
+import { TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "../core/tools.js";
+import { logger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import { SERVER } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import { startHttpServer } from "./cli-http.js";
+
+export interface CliParsedArgs {
+  help: boolean;
+  version: boolean;
+  transport?: "stdio" | "http";
+  port?: number;
+  host?: string;
+}
+
+export function parseCliArgs(argv: string[]): CliParsedArgs {
+  const parsed: CliParsedArgs = { help: false, version: false };
+
+  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
+    const arg = argv[i];
+    if (arg === "--help" || arg === "-h") {
+      parsed.help = true;
+    } else if (arg === "--version" || arg === "-v") {
+      parsed.version = true;
+    } else if (arg === "--http") {
+      parsed.transport = "http";
+    } else if (arg === "--stdio") {
+      parsed.transport = "stdio";
+    } else if (arg === "--port" && i + 1 < argv.length) {
+      const p = Number(argv[++i]);
+      if (Number.isInteger(p) && p >= 0 && p <= 65535) {
+        parsed.port = p;
+      }
+    } else if (arg.startsWith("--port=")) {
+      const p = Number(arg.slice("--port=".length));
+      if (Number.isInteger(p) && p >= 0 && p <= 65535) {
+        parsed.port = p;
+      }
+    } else if (arg === "--host" && i + 1 < argv.length) {
+      parsed.host = argv[++i];
+    } else if (arg.startsWith("--host=")) {
+      parsed.host = arg.slice("--host=".length);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return parsed;
+}
+
+export function printHelp(): void {
+  const helpText = `${SERVER.NAME} v${SERVER.VERSION}
+MCP Server for Brazilian Public Budget & Fiscal Data (SICONFI, Transferegov, SADIPEM/CKAN)
+
+Usage:
+  mcp-orcamento [options]
+
+Options:
+  --stdio             Start over stdio transport (default)
+  --http              Start over hardened Express HTTP/SSE transport
+  --port <port>       HTTP listen port (default: ${SERVER.DEFAULT_HTTP_PORT})
+  --host <host>       HTTP bind host (default: 127.0.0.1)
+  -v, --version       Print version and exit
+  -h, --help          Show this help message
+
+Environment:
+  MCP_TRANSPORT              stdio | http
+  MCP_HTTP_PORT              HTTP server port
+  MCP_HTTP_HOST              HTTP bind host (default 127.0.0.1)
+  MCP_API_KEY                Optional API key for HTTP transport
+  TESOURO_ORDS_BASE_URL      Tesouro ORDS override
+  TRANSFEREGOV_BASE_URL      Transferegov API override
+  TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL      Tesouro Transparente CKAN override
+`;
+  console.error(helpText);
+}
+
+export async function runCli(argv: string[] = process.argv.slice(2)): Promise<void> {
+  const cliArgs = parseCliArgs(argv);
+
+  if (cliArgs.help) {
+    printHelp();
+    return;
+  }
+
+  if (cliArgs.version) {
+    console.log(`${SERVER.NAME} ${SERVER.VERSION}`);
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const { apiConfig, serverConfig } = loadServerConfiguration();
+  const effectiveTransport = cliArgs.transport ?? serverConfig.transport;
+  if (cliArgs.port !== undefined) {
+    serverConfig.httpPort = cliArgs.port;
+  }
+  if (cliArgs.host !== undefined) {
+    serverConfig.httpHost = cliArgs.host;
+  }
+
+  const cache = new MemoryCache(serverConfig.cacheSize, serverConfig.cacheTTL);
+
+  logger.info("Starting MCP Orçamento server", {
+    service: SERVER.NAME,
+    version: SERVER.VERSION,
+    transport: effectiveTransport,
+    tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS.length,
+    tesouroOrdsBaseUrl: apiConfig.tesouroOrdsBaseUrl,
+    transferegovBaseUrl: apiConfig.transferegovBaseUrl,
+    tesouroCkanBaseUrl: apiConfig.tesouroCkanBaseUrl,
+  });
+
+  if (effectiveTransport === "http") {
+    const running = await startHttpServer({ apiConfig, serverConfig, cache });
+    const shutdown = async (signal: string) => {
+      logger.info("Shutting down HTTP MCP server", { signal });
+      await running.close();
+      process.exit(0);
+    };
+    process.once("SIGINT", () => void shutdown("SIGINT"));
+    process.once("SIGTERM", () => void shutdown("SIGTERM"));
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const mcpServer = createMCPServer({
+    apiConfig,
+    cache,
+    transportName: "stdio",
+  });
+  const transport = new StdioServerTransport();
+
+  let closing = false;
+  const shutdownStdio = async (reason: string) => {
+    if (closing) return;
+    closing = true;
+    logger.debug("Closing stdio MCP server", { reason });
+    await mcpServer.close().catch(() => {});
+    cache.destroy();
+  };
+
+  process.once("SIGINT", () => {
+    void shutdownStdio("SIGINT").finally(() => process.exit(0));
+  });
+  process.once("SIGTERM", () => {
+    void shutdownStdio("SIGTERM").finally(() => process.exit(0));
+  });
+  process.stdin.once("end", () => {
+    void shutdownStdio("stdin-end");
+  });
+
+  await mcpServer.connect(transport);
+}
+
+function isExecutedDirectly(): boolean {
+  if (typeof process === "undefined" || !process.argv?.[1]) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  try {
+    const invoked = realpathSync(path.resolve(process.argv[1]));
+    const current = realpathSync(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+    return invoked === current || invoked.endsWith("mcp-orcamento.js");
+  } catch {
+    return false;
+  }
+}
+
+if (isExecutedDirectly()) {
+  runCli().catch((error) => {
+    logger.error("Fatal CLI error", {
+      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+    });
+    process.exit(1);
+  });
+}

+++ b/lib/adapters/cloudflare.ts

+/**
+ * Cloudflare Worker adapter bridging Web Requests / JSON-RPC to the shared 8-tool registry.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports.
+ */
+
+import { KVCache, MemoryCache } from "../core/cache.js";
+import { executeTool, getMetrics, hasTool, TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "../core/tools.js";
+import { logger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import {
+  createRequestContext,
+  REQUEST_ID_HEADER,
+  type RequestContext,
+} from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import {
+  resolveMaxToolOutputChars,
+  serializeBoundedToolOutput,
+} from "../shared/agentic-guard.js";
+import { ErrorCode, formatErrorResponse } from "../shared/errors.js";
+import {
+  ALLOWED_TESOURO_CKAN_HOSTS,
+  ALLOWED_TESOURO_ORDS_HOSTS,
+  ALLOWED_TRANSFEREGOV_HOSTS,
+  API_URLS,
+  CACHE,
+  SERVER,
+  validateUrl,
+} from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import type { ApiConfig, Cache, MCPRequest, MCPResponse } from "../types/index.js";
+import type { WorkerEnv } from "../workers/types.js";
+
+const MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES = 100_000;
+const SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS = ["2025-11-25", "2025-03-26", "2024-11-05"] as const;
+
+let workerMemoryCache: MemoryCache | undefined;
+
+function parsePositiveInt(raw: string | undefined, fallback: number): number {
+  if (typeof raw !== "string" || !/^\d+$/.test(raw.trim())) {
+    return fallback;
+  }
+  const n = Number(raw.trim());
+  return Number.isSafeInteger(n) && n > 0 ? n : fallback;
+}
+
+export function resolveWorkerApiConfig(env: WorkerEnv): ApiConfig {
+  const allowLoopback = env.MCP_ALLOW_LOCALHOST_APIS === "true";
+
+  const tesouroOrdsBaseUrl = validateUrl(
+    env.TESOURO_ORDS_BASE_URL || API_URLS.DEFAULT_TESOURO_ORDS,
+    { allowedHosts: ALLOWED_TESOURO_ORDS_HOSTS, allowLoopback, env: allowLoopback ? "test" : "production" }
+  );
+  const transferegovBaseUrl = validateUrl(
+    env.TRANSFEREGOV_BASE_URL || API_URLS.DEFAULT_TRANSFEREGOV,
+    { allowedHosts: ALLOWED_TRANSFEREGOV_HOSTS, allowLoopback, env: allowLoopback ? "test" : "production" }
+  );
+  const tesouroCkanBaseUrl = validateUrl(
+    env.TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL || API_URLS.DEFAULT_TESOURO_CKAN,
+    { allowedHosts: ALLOWED_TESOURO_CKAN_HOSTS, allowLoopback, env: allowLoopback ? "test" : "production" }
+  );
+
+  return {
+    tesouroOrdsBaseUrl,
+    transferegovBaseUrl,
+    tesouroCkanBaseUrl,
+  };
+}
+
+export function buildWorkerCache(env: WorkerEnv): Cache {
+  const ttlMs = parsePositiveInt(env.MCP_CACHE_TTL, CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS);
+  const maxSize = parsePositiveInt(env.MCP_CACHE_SIZE, CACHE.DEFAULT_SIZE);
+
+  const kv = env.MCP_CACHE ?? env.MCP_KV;
+  if (kv) {
+    return new KVCache(kv, ttlMs);
+  }
+
+  if (!workerMemoryCache) {
+    // cleanupIntervalMs=0 avoids forbidden global setInterval in Workers
+    workerMemoryCache = new MemoryCache(maxSize, ttlMs, 0);
+  }
+  return workerMemoryCache;
+}
+
+export function resetWorkerMemoryCache(): void {
+  workerMemoryCache?.clear();
+  workerMemoryCache = undefined;
+}
+
+export function getCorsHeaders(
+  request: Request | undefined,
+  env: WorkerEnv
+): Record<string, string> {
+  const baseHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
+    "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, OPTIONS",
+    "Access-Control-Allow-Headers":
+      "Content-Type, Authorization, X-API-Key, X-Request-Id, Mcp-Session-Id",
+    "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400",
+  };
+
+  const allowedRaw = env.CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS?.trim();
+  if (!allowedRaw) {
+    return {
+      ...baseHeaders,
+      "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
+    };
+  }
+
+  const allowedOrigins = allowedRaw
+    .split(",")
+    .map((o) => o.trim())
+    .filter((o) => o.length > 0);
+  const requestOrigin = request?.headers.get("origin")?.trim();
+
+  if (requestOrigin && allowedOrigins.includes(requestOrigin)) {
+    return {
+      ...baseHeaders,
+      "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": requestOrigin,
+      Vary: "Origin",
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ...baseHeaders,
+    Vary: "Origin",
+  };
+}
+
+export function handleOptions(request: Request, env: WorkerEnv): Response {
+  return new Response(null, {
+    status: 204,
+    headers: getCorsHeaders(request, env),
+  });
+}
+
+function negotiateProtocolVersion(requested?: unknown): string {
+  if (
+    typeof requested === "string" &&
+    (SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS as readonly string[]).includes(requested)
+  ) {
+    return requested;
+  }
+  return SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS[0];
+}
+
+export async function handleMCPRequest(
+  rpcRequest: MCPRequest,
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext
+): Promise<MCPResponse> {
+  const id = rpcRequest?.id ?? null;
+
+  if (!rpcRequest || rpcRequest.jsonrpc !== "2.0" || typeof rpcRequest.method !== "string") {
+    return {
+      jsonrpc: "2.0",
+      id,
+      error: {
+        code: ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
+        message: "Invalid JSON-RPC 2.0 request",
+      },
+    };
+  }
+
+  const reqCtx = requestContext ?? createRequestContext({ transport: "cloudflare-worker" });
+
+  try {
+    switch (rpcRequest.method) {
+      case "initialize": {
+        const params =
+          typeof rpcRequest.params === "object" && rpcRequest.params !== null
+            ? (rpcRequest.params as { protocolVersion?: unknown })
+            : {};
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id,
+          result: {
+            protocolVersion: negotiateProtocolVersion(params.protocolVersion),
+            capabilities: {
+              tools: {},
+              resources: {},
+            },
+            serverInfo: {
+              name: SERVER.NAME,
+              version: SERVER.VERSION,
+            },
+          },
+        };
+      }
+
+      case "notifications/initialized":
+      case "ping": {
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id,
+          result: {},
+        };
+      }
+
+      case "tools/list": {
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id,
+          result: {
+            tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
+          },
+        };
+      }
+
+      case "resources/list": {
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id,
+          result: {
+            resources: [],
+          },
+        };
+      }
+
+      case "resources/read": {
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id,
+          error: {
+            code: ErrorCode.INVALID_PARAMS,
+            message: "Unknown resource URI",
+          },
+        };
+      }
+
+      case "tools/call": {
+        const params =
+          typeof rpcRequest.params === "object" && rpcRequest.params !== null
+            ? (rpcRequest.params as { name?: unknown; arguments?: unknown })
+            : {};
+
+        if (typeof params.name !== "string" || params.name.trim().length === 0) {
+          return {
+            jsonrpc: "2.0",
+            id,
+            error: {
+              code: ErrorCode.INVALID_PARAMS,
+              message: "tools/call requires a non-empty string 'name' parameter",
+            },
+          };
+        }
+
+        const apiConfig = resolveWorkerApiConfig(env);
+        const cache = buildWorkerCache(env);
+        const maxChars = resolveMaxToolOutputChars(env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS);
+
+        const result = await executeTool(params.name, params.arguments ?? {}, {
+          apiConfig,
+          cache,
+          requestId: reqCtx.requestId,
+          requestContext: reqCtx,
+        });
+
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id,
+          result: {
+            isError: !result.ok,
+            content: [
+              {
+                type: "text",
+                text: serializeBoundedToolOutput(result, maxChars),
+              },
+            ],
+          },
+        };
+      }
+
+      default: {
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id,
+          error: {
+            code: ErrorCode.METHOD_NOT_FOUND,
+            message: `Method not found: ${rpcRequest.method}`,
+          },
+        };
+      }
+    }
+  } catch (error) {
+    logger.error("Worker MCP request execution failed", {
+      method: rpcRequest.method,
+      requestId: reqCtx.requestId,
+      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+    });
+    return {
+      jsonrpc: "2.0",
+      id,
+      error: formatErrorResponse(error),
+    };
+  }
+}
+
+export async function readBoundedJsonBody(request: Request): Promise<
+  | { ok: true; body: unknown; rawText: string }
+  | { ok: false; status: number; error: string; code: number }
+> {
+  const buffer = await request.arrayBuffer();
+  if (buffer.byteLength > MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      status: 413,
+      code: ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
+      error: `Request body exceeds ${MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES} bytes limit`,
+    };
+  }
+
+  let rawText: string;
+  try {
+    rawText = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(buffer);
+  } catch {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      status: 400,
+      code: ErrorCode.PARSE_ERROR,
+      error: "Invalid UTF-8 request body encoding",
+    };
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const body: unknown = JSON.parse(rawText);
+    return { ok: true, body, rawText };
+  } catch {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      status: 400,
+      code: ErrorCode.PARSE_ERROR,
+      error: "Malformed JSON request body",
+    };
+  }
+}
+
+export async function handleMCPEndpoint(
+  request: Request,
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext
+): Promise<Response> {
+  const reqCtx =
+    requestContext ?? createRequestContext({ headers: request.headers, transport: "worker-http" });
+  const corsHeaders = getCorsHeaders(request, env);
+  const baseHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
+    "Content-Type": "application/json",
+    [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+    ...corsHeaders,
+  };
+
+  if (request.method !== "POST") {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: null,
+        error: { code: ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST, message: "Method not allowed; use POST" },
+      }),
+      { status: 405, headers: baseHeaders }
+    );
+  }
+
+  const parsed = await readBoundedJsonBody(request);
+  if (!parsed.ok) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: null,
+        error: { code: parsed.code, message: parsed.error },
+      }),
+      { status: parsed.status, headers: baseHeaders }
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (typeof parsed.body !== "object" || parsed.body === null || Array.isArray(parsed.body)) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: null,
+        error: { code: ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST, message: "Expected JSON-RPC object payload" },
+      }),
+      { status: 400, headers: baseHeaders }
+    );
+  }
+
+  const rpcRequest = parsed.body as MCPRequest;
+  const response = await handleMCPRequest(rpcRequest, env, reqCtx);
+
+  const isNotification =
+    rpcRequest.id === undefined &&
+    typeof rpcRequest.method === "string" &&
+    rpcRequest.method.startsWith("notifications/");
+
+  if (isNotification) {
+    return new Response(null, { status: 202, headers: baseHeaders });
+  }
+
+  return new Response(JSON.stringify(response), {
+    status: 200,
+    headers: baseHeaders,
+  });
+}
+
+export async function handleHealthCheck(
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  request?: Request,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext
+): Promise<Response> {
+  const reqCtx =
+    requestContext ?? createRequestContext({ headers: request?.headers, transport: "worker-health" });
+  const metrics = getMetrics();
+  const corsHeaders = getCorsHeaders(request, env);
+
+  return new Response(
+    JSON.stringify({
+      status: "healthy",
+      service: SERVER.NAME,
+      version: SERVER.VERSION,
+      runtime: "cloudflare-workers",
+      tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS.length,
+      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
+      kv_bound: Boolean(env.MCP_CACHE || env.MCP_KV),
+      privacy: {
+        dpo_contact: env.MCP_DPO_CONTACT ?? null,
+        lgpd_notice: true,
+      },
+      metrics: {
+        requests: metrics.requests,
+        cacheHits: metrics.cacheHits,
+        errors: metrics.errors,
+        uptimeMs: metrics.uptimeMs,
+      },
+    }),
+    {
+      status: 200,
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+        ...corsHeaders,
+      },
+    }
+  );
+}

+/**
+ * Node configuration loader (.mcprc.json + env) and SSRF-safe API config resolution.
+ */
+
+import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import os from "node:os";
+import path from "node:path";
+import { ConfigurationError } from "../shared/errors.js";
+import {
+  ALLOWED_TESOURO_CKAN_HOSTS,
+  ALLOWED_TESOURO_ORDS_HOSTS,
+  ALLOWED_TRANSFEREGOV_HOSTS,
+  API_URLS,
+  CACHE,
+  isAllowedTesouroCkanUrl,
+  isAllowedTesouroOrdsUrl,
+  isAllowedTransferegovUrl,
+  isLoopbackHost,
+  SERVER,
+  validateUrl,
+  type ValidateUrlOptions,
+} from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import type { ApiConfig, ConfigFile, ServerConfig } from "../types/index.js";
+import { parsePositiveTimeoutMs, TIMEOUTS } from "./timeouts.js";
+
+export const SERVER_NAME = SERVER.NAME;
+export const SERVER_VERSION = SERVER.VERSION;
+
+export const DEFAULT_TESOURO_ORDS_URL = API_URLS.DEFAULT_TESOURO_ORDS;
+export const DEFAULT_TRANSFEREGOV_URL = API_URLS.DEFAULT_TRANSFEREGOV;
+export const DEFAULT_TESOURO_CKAN_URL = API_URLS.DEFAULT_TESOURO_CKAN;
+
+export {
+  ALLOWED_TESOURO_CKAN_HOSTS,
+  ALLOWED_TESOURO_ORDS_HOSTS,
+  ALLOWED_TRANSFEREGOV_HOSTS,
+  isAllowedTesouroCkanUrl,
+  isAllowedTesouroOrdsUrl,
+  isAllowedTransferegovUrl,
+  isLoopbackHost,
+  validateUrl,
+  type ValidateUrlOptions,
+};
+
+export interface ApiConfigOverrides {
+  tesouroOrdsBaseUrl?: string;
+  transferegovBaseUrl?: string;
+  tesouroCkanBaseUrl?: string;
+  authHeaders?: Record<string, string>;
+  configFilePath?: string;
+  allowLoopback?: boolean;
+  env?: string;
+}
+
+function getEnv(key: string): string | undefined {
+  if (typeof process === "undefined" || !process?.env) {
+    return undefined;
+  }
+  return process.env[key];
+}
+
+export function loadConfigFile(customPath?: string): ConfigFile {
+  const candidates: string[] = [];
+  if (customPath) {
+    candidates.push(customPath);
+  } else {
+    const envPath = getEnv("MCPRC_PATH");
+    if (envPath) {
+      candidates.push(envPath);
+    }
+    if (typeof process !== "undefined" && typeof process.cwd === "function") {
+      candidates.push(path.join(process.cwd(), ".mcprc.json"));
+    }
+    if (typeof os.homedir === "function") {
+      candidates.push(path.join(os.homedir(), ".mcprc.json"));
+    }
+  }
+
+  for (const filePath of candidates) {
+    try {
+      if (!existsSync(filePath)) {
+        continue;
+      }
+      const raw = readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
+      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
+      if (typeof parsed !== "object" || parsed === null || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
+        throw new ConfigurationError(`Invalid .mcprc.json structure in ${filePath}`);
+      }
+      return parsed as ConfigFile;
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (error instanceof ConfigurationError) {
+        throw error;
+      }
+      throw new ConfigurationError(
+        `Failed to parse config file ${filePath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  return {};
+}
+
+export function resolveApiConfig(overrides: ApiConfigOverrides = {}): ApiConfig {
+  const fileConfig = overrides.configFilePath
+    ? loadConfigFile(overrides.configFilePath)
+    : typeof process !== "undefined"
+      ? loadConfigFile()
+      : {};
+
+  const rawTesouroOrds =
+    overrides.tesouroOrdsBaseUrl ??
+    getEnv("TESOURO_ORDS_BASE_URL") ??
+    fileConfig.apiUrls?.tesouroOrds ??
+    DEFAULT_TESOURO_ORDS_URL;
+
+  const rawTransferegov =
+    overrides.transferegovBaseUrl ??
+    getEnv("TRANSFEREGOV_BASE_URL") ??
+    fileConfig.apiUrls?.transferegov ??
+    DEFAULT_TRANSFEREGOV_URL;
+
+  const rawTesouroCkan =
+    overrides.tesouroCkanBaseUrl ??
+    getEnv("TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL") ??
+    fileConfig.apiUrls?.tesouroCkan ??
+    DEFAULT_TESOURO_CKAN_URL;
+
+  const urlOpts: Omit<ValidateUrlOptions, "allowedHosts"> = {
+    allowLoopback: overrides.allowLoopback,
+    env: overrides.env,
+  };
+
+  const tesouroOrdsBaseUrl = validateUrl(rawTesouroOrds, {
+    ...urlOpts,
+    allowedHosts: ALLOWED_TESOURO_ORDS_HOSTS,
+  });
+  const transferegovBaseUrl = validateUrl(rawTransferegov, {
+    ...urlOpts,
+    allowedHosts: ALLOWED_TRANSFEREGOV_HOSTS,
+  });
+  const tesouroCkanBaseUrl = validateUrl(rawTesouroCkan, {
+    ...urlOpts,
+    allowedHosts: ALLOWED_TESOURO_CKAN_HOSTS,
+  });
+
+  const mergedHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
+    ...(fileConfig.auth?.headers ?? {}),
+  };
+
+  const apiKeyHeader = getEnv("API_KEY_HEADER");
+  const apiKeyValue = getEnv("API_KEY_VALUE");
+  if (apiKeyHeader && apiKeyValue) {
+    mergedHeaders[apiKeyHeader] = apiKeyValue;
+  }
+
+  if (overrides.authHeaders) {
+    Object.assign(mergedHeaders, overrides.authHeaders);
+  }
+
+  return {
+    tesouroOrdsBaseUrl,
+    transferegovBaseUrl,
+    tesouroCkanBaseUrl,
+    ...(Object.keys(mergedHeaders).length > 0 ? { authHeaders: mergedHeaders } : {}),
+  };
+}
+
+export function loadServerConfiguration(overrides: ApiConfigOverrides = {}): {
+  apiConfig: ApiConfig;
+  serverConfig: ServerConfig;
+} {
+  const apiConfig = resolveApiConfig(overrides);
+
+  const rawPort = getEnv("MCP_HTTP_PORT");
+  const parsedPort =
+    rawPort && /^\d+$/.test(rawPort.trim()) ? Number(rawPort.trim()) : SERVER.DEFAULT_HTTP_PORT;
+  const httpPort = parsedPort >= 1 && parsedPort <= 65535 ? parsedPort : SERVER.DEFAULT_HTTP_PORT;
+
+  const cacheSize = parsePositiveTimeoutMs(getEnv("MCP_CACHE_SIZE"), CACHE.DEFAULT_SIZE);
+  const cacheTTL = parsePositiveTimeoutMs(getEnv("MCP_CACHE_TTL"), CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS);
+  const apiTimeout = parsePositiveTimeoutMs(getEnv("MCP_API_TIMEOUT"), TIMEOUTS.HTTP_REQUEST_MS);
+
+  const transport = (getEnv("MCP_TRANSPORT") || SERVER.DEFAULT_TRANSPORT).trim().toLowerCase();
+  const httpHost = getEnv("MCP_HTTP_HOST")?.trim() || undefined;
+
+  return {
+    apiConfig,
+    serverConfig: {
+      transport,
+      httpPort,
+      ...(httpHost ? { httpHost } : {}),
+      cacheSize,
+      cacheTTL,
+      apiTimeout,
+    },
+  };
+}

+/**
+ * Centralized timeout configuration for HTTP, ORDS, SSE, circuit breaker, and rate limiting.
+ * Zero Node-only imports so Cloudflare Workers and Node CLI share identical defaults.
+ */
+
+function readEnvString(key: string): string | undefined {
+  if (typeof process === "undefined" || !process?.env) {
+    return undefined;
+  }
+  return process.env[key];
+}
+
+export function parsePositiveTimeoutMs(raw: string | undefined, fallbackMs: number): number {
+  if (typeof raw !== "string") {
+    return fallbackMs;
+  }
+  const trimmed = raw.trim();
+  if (!/^\d+$/.test(trimmed)) {
+    return fallbackMs;
+  }
+  const parsed = Number(trimmed);
+  if (Number.isSafeInteger(parsed) && parsed > 0) {
+    return parsed;
+  }
+  return fallbackMs;
+}
+
+export function getOrdsRequestTimeoutMs(override?: string): number {
+  const raw = override !== undefined ? override : readEnvString("MCP_ORDS_TIMEOUT");
+  return parsePositiveTimeoutMs(raw, 15_000);
+}
+
+export const TIMEOUTS = {
+  /** Standard HTTP timeout for fast APIs (CKAN, Transferegov PostgREST) */
+  HTTP_REQUEST_MS: parsePositiveTimeoutMs(readEnvString("MCP_HTTP_TIMEOUT"), 8_000),
+
+  /** Extended HTTP timeout for Tesouro Nacional ORDS endpoints (SICONFI / SADIPEM) */
+  ORDS_REQUEST_MS: getOrdsRequestTimeoutMs(),
+
+  /** Cooldown before transitioning an open circuit breaker to HALF_OPEN */
+  CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS: 30_000,
+
+  /** Maximum lifetime for an SSE HTTP connection */
+  SSE_CONNECTION_MS: 50_000,
+
+  /** Heartbeat ping interval for active SSE connections */
+  PING_INTERVAL_MS: 30_000,
+
+  /** Interval between expired sliding-window rate-limiter bucket cleanups */
+  RATE_LIMIT_CLEANUP_MS: 60_000,
+
+  /** Sliding window duration for rate limiting */
+  RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS: 60_000,
+} as const;

+/**
+ * Cache implementations for Node (in-memory LRU + TTL) and Cloudflare Workers (KV).
+ * Zero `node:*` imports.
+ */
+
+import { CACHE } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import type { Cache, CacheEntry } from "../types/index.js";
+
+export const MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS = 60;
+
+export interface KVNamespaceLike {
+  get(key: string, type: "json"): Promise<unknown>;
+  put(key: string, value: string, options?: { expirationTtl?: number }): Promise<void>;
+}
+
+export class MemoryCache implements Cache {
+  private readonly cache = new Map<string, CacheEntry>();
+  private readonly maxSize: number;
+  private readonly ttlMs: number;
+  private cleanupTimer?: ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;
+
+  constructor(
+    maxSize: number = CACHE.DEFAULT_SIZE,
+    ttlMs: number = CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS,
+    cleanupIntervalMs: number = CACHE.CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS
+  ) {
+    this.maxSize = maxSize > 0 ? Math.floor(maxSize) : CACHE.DEFAULT_SIZE;
+    this.ttlMs = ttlMs > 0 ? Math.floor(ttlMs) : CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS;
+
+    if (cleanupIntervalMs > 0 && typeof setInterval === "function") {
+      this.cleanupTimer = setInterval(() => this.cleanup(), cleanupIntervalMs);
+      (this.cleanupTimer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
+    }
+  }
+
+  get<T = unknown>(key: string): T | null {
+    const entry = this.cache.get(key);
+    if (!entry) {
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    if (Date.now() > entry.expires) {
+      this.cache.delete(key);
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    // Refresh insertion order for LRU eviction
+    this.cache.delete(key);
+    this.cache.set(key, entry);
+    return entry.data as T;
+  }
+
+  set<T = unknown>(key: string, data: T): void {
+    if (this.cache.has(key)) {
+      this.cache.delete(key);
+    } else if (this.cache.size >= this.maxSize) {
+      const oldestKey = this.cache.keys().next().value;
+      if (oldestKey !== undefined) {
+        this.cache.delete(oldestKey);
+      }
+    }
+
+    this.cache.set(key, {
+      data,
+      expires: Date.now() + this.ttlMs,
+    });
+  }
+
+  cleanup(now: number = Date.now()): number {
+    let removed = 0;
+    for (const [key, entry] of this.cache.entries()) {
+      if (now > entry.expires) {
+        this.cache.delete(key);
+        removed++;
+      }
+    }
+    return removed;
+  }
+
+  clear(): void {
+    this.cache.clear();
+  }
+
+  size(): number {
+    return this.cache.size;
+  }
+
+  destroy(): void {
+    if (this.cleanupTimer !== undefined) {
+      clearInterval(this.cleanupTimer);
+      this.cleanupTimer = undefined;
+    }
+    this.cache.clear();
+  }
+
+  dispose(): void {
+    this.destroy();
+  }
+}
+
+export class KVCache implements Cache {
+  private readonly ttlSeconds: number;
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly kv: KVNamespaceLike,
+    ttlMs: number = CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS
+  ) {
+    const computed = Math.ceil((ttlMs > 0 ? ttlMs : CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS) / 1000);
+    this.ttlSeconds = Math.max(MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS, computed);
+  }
+
+  async get<T = unknown>(key: string): Promise<T | null> {
+    try {
+      const value = await this.kv.get(key, "json");
+      return (value as T | null | undefined) ?? null;
+    } catch {
+      return null;
+    }
+  }
+
+  async set<T = unknown>(key: string, data: T): Promise<void> {
+    try {
+      await this.kv.put(key, JSON.stringify(data), {
+        expirationTtl: this.ttlSeconds,
+      });
+    } catch {
+      // Transient KV write failure should not break tool execution
+    }
+  }
+
+  async clear(): Promise<void> {
+    // KV namespace clearing is intentionally unsupported in Workers runtime
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Resilient Web-standard HTTP JSON client with per-origin Circuit Breaker,
+ * equal-jitter retries, and strict AbortSignal timeout cleanup.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports for Cloudflare Workers parity.
+ */
+
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../config/timeouts.js";
+import {
+  CircuitBreaker,
+  CircuitBreakerOpenError,
+} from "../infrastructure/http/circuit-breaker.js";
+import { logger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import { REQUEST_ID_HEADER } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import {
+  createTimeoutController,
+  isAbortOrTimeoutError,
+} from "../shared/utils/timeout.js";
+import type { HttpResponse } from "../types/index.js";
+
+const originBreakers = new Map<string, CircuitBreaker>();
+
+function getOriginKey(url: string): string {
+  try {
+    return new URL(url).origin;
+  } catch {
+    return "unknown";
+  }
+}
+
+export function getCircuitBreakerForOrigin(origin: string): CircuitBreaker {
+  let breaker = originBreakers.get(origin);
+  if (!breaker) {
+    breaker = new CircuitBreaker({ service: origin });
+    originBreakers.set(origin, breaker);
+  }
+  return breaker;
+}
+
+export function resetCircuitBreakers(): void {
+  for (const breaker of originBreakers.values()) {
+    breaker.reset();
+  }
+  originBreakers.clear();
+}
+
+export function getCircuitBreakerMetrics() {
+  return Array.from(originBreakers.values()).map((b) => b.getMetrics());
+}
+
+export interface HttpClientOptions {
+  timeout?: number;
+  retries?: number;
+  baseDelayMs?: number;
+  headers?: Record<string, string>;
+  signal?: AbortSignal;
+  requestId?: string;
+  circuitBreaker?: CircuitBreaker;
+}
+
+class RetryableHttpError extends Error {
+  constructor(
+    message: string,
+    readonly statusCode?: number
+  ) {
+    super(message);
+    this.name = "RetryableHttpError";
+  }
+}
+
+function sleepWithSignal(ms: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
+  if (ms <= 0) return Promise.resolve();
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    if (signal?.aborted) {
+      reject(new Error("Request aborted"));
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const onAbort = () => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      reject(new Error("Request aborted"));
+    };
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      if (signal) {
+        signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
+      }
+      resolve();
+    }, ms);
+
+    if (signal) {
+      signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
+    }
+  });
+}
+
+function equalJitterDelayMs(baseDelayMs: number, attempt: number): number {
+  const cap = baseDelayMs * Math.pow(2, attempt);
+  const half = cap / 2;
+  return Math.floor(half + Math.random() * half);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Fetch JSON with per-origin circuit breaking, bounded timeouts, and retry backoff.
+ * Non-retryable 4xx responses return { ok: false } without tripping the circuit breaker.
+ */
+export async function httpJson(
+  url: string,
+  options: HttpClientOptions = {}
+): Promise<HttpResponse> {
+  const timeoutMs =
+    typeof options.timeout === "number" && options.timeout > 0
+      ? options.timeout
+      : TIMEOUTS.HTTP_REQUEST_MS;
+  const maxRetries =
+    typeof options.retries === "number" && options.retries >= 0
+      ? Math.floor(options.retries)
+      : 2;
+  const baseDelayMs =
+    typeof options.baseDelayMs === "number" && options.baseDelayMs >= 0
+      ? options.baseDelayMs
+      : 250;
+
+  if (options.signal?.aborted) {
+    return { ok: false, error: "Request aborted by caller", source: url };
+  }
+
+  const origin = getOriginKey(url);
+  const breaker = options.circuitBreaker ?? getCircuitBreakerForOrigin(origin);
+
+  let nonRetryableResponse: HttpResponse | undefined;
+
+  try {
+    return await breaker.execute(async () => {
+      let lastError: unknown;
+
+      for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++) {
+        const ctrl = createTimeoutController(timeoutMs, options.signal);
+
+        try {
+          const headers: Record<string, string> = {
+            Accept: "application/json",
+            "User-Agent": "mcp-orcamento/0.1.0",
+            ...(options.headers ?? {}),
+          };
+
+          if (options.requestId) {
+            headers[REQUEST_ID_HEADER] = options.requestId;
+          }
+
+          const response = await fetch(url, {
+            method: "GET",
+            headers,
+            signal: ctrl.signal,
+            redirect: "error",
+          });
+
+          if (!response.ok) {
+            const status = response.status;
+            const statusText = response.statusText || "HTTP Error";
+
+            // Retry transient 429 & 5xx responses
+            if (status === 429 || status >= 500) {
+              throw new RetryableHttpError(`HTTP ${status} ${statusText}`, status);
+            }
+
+            // 4xx (except 429) should not trip upstream availability circuit breaker
+            nonRetryableResponse = {
+              ok: false,
+              error: `HTTP ${status} ${statusText}`,
+              source: url,
+            };
+            return nonRetryableResponse;
+          }
+
+          const rawText = await response.text();
+          let parsed: unknown;
+          try {
+            parsed = rawText.length > 0 ? JSON.parse(rawText) : null;
+          } catch {
+            nonRetryableResponse = {
+              ok: false,
+              error: "Invalid JSON response from upstream API",
+              source: url,
+            };
+            return nonRetryableResponse;
+          }
+
+          return {
+            ok: true,
+            data: parsed,
+            source: url,
+          };
+        } catch (error) {
+          if (ctrl.wasAbortedByParent() || options.signal?.aborted) {
+            nonRetryableResponse = {
+              ok: false,
+              error: "Request aborted by caller",
+              source: url,
+            };
+            return nonRetryableResponse;
+          }
+
+          if (ctrl.didTimeout() || isAbortOrTimeoutError(error)) {
+            lastError = new RetryableHttpError(`Request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`);
+          } else {
+            lastError = error;
+          }
+
+          if (attempt < maxRetries) {
+            const delayMs = equalJitterDelayMs(baseDelayMs, attempt);
+            logger.debug("Retrying upstream HTTP request", {
+              origin,
+              attempt: attempt + 1,
+              delayMs,
+              requestId: options.requestId,
+            });
+            try {
+              await sleepWithSignal(delayMs, options.signal);
+            } catch {
+              nonRetryableResponse = {
+                ok: false,
+                error: "Request aborted by caller",
+                source: url,
+              };
+              return nonRetryableResponse;
+            }
+          }
+        } finally {
+          ctrl.cleanup();
+        }
+      }
+
+      throw lastError instanceof Error
+        ? lastError
+        : new Error(String(lastError ?? "Upstream HTTP request failed"));
+    });
+  } catch (error) {
+    if (nonRetryableResponse) {
+      return nonRetryableResponse;
+    }
+
+    if (error instanceof CircuitBreakerOpenError) {
+      return {
+        ok: false,
+        error: error.message,
+        source: url,
+      };
+    }
+
+    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      error: message,
+      source: url,
+    };
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Shared MCP Protocol Server factory for stdio and HTTP/SSE transports.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports. Kept concise (<= 180 lines).
+ */
+
+import { Server } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js";
+import {
+  CallToolRequestSchema,
+  ErrorCode,
+  ListResourcesRequestSchema,
+  ListToolsRequestSchema,
+  McpError,
+  ReadResourceRequestSchema,
+} from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js";
+import { logger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import { RateLimiter } from "../infrastructure/rate-limiter.js";
+import { createRequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { serializeBoundedToolOutput } from "../shared/agentic-guard.js";
+import { API_URLS, SERVER } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import type { ApiConfig, Cache } from "../types/index.js";
+import { MemoryCache } from "./cache.js";
+import { executeTool, getMetrics, TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "./tools.js";
+
+export interface CreateMCPServerOptions {
+  apiConfig?: ApiConfig;
+  cache?: Cache;
+  rateLimiter?: RateLimiter;
+  rateLimitKey?: string;
+  maxToolOutputChars?: number;
+  maxRequestsPerMinute?: number;
+  transportName?: string;
+}
+
+function defaultApiConfig(): ApiConfig {
+  return {
+    tesouroOrdsBaseUrl: API_URLS.DEFAULT_TESOURO_ORDS,
+    transferegovBaseUrl: API_URLS.DEFAULT_TRANSFEREGOV,
+    tesouroCkanBaseUrl: API_URLS.DEFAULT_TESOURO_CKAN,
+  };
+}
+
+export function createMCPServer(options: CreateMCPServerOptions = {}): Server {
+  const apiConfig = options.apiConfig ?? defaultApiConfig();
+  const ownsCache = !options.cache;
+  const cache: Cache = options.cache ?? new MemoryCache();
+  const ownsRateLimiter = !options.rateLimiter;
+  const rateLimiter =
+    options.rateLimiter ??
+    new RateLimiter({
+      maxRequests: options.maxRequestsPerMinute ?? 100,
+      windowMs: 60_000,
+      cleanupIntervalMs: 60_000,
+    });
+  const sessionKey = options.rateLimitKey ?? "mcp-session";
+  const transportName = options.transportName ?? "mcp";
+
+  const server = new Server(
+    {
+      name: SERVER.NAME,
+      version: SERVER.VERSION,
+    },
+    {
+      capabilities: {
+        tools: {},
+        resources: {},
+      },
+    }
+  );
+
+  server.setRequestHandler(ListToolsRequestSchema, async () => ({
+    tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS.map((tool) => ({
+      name: tool.name,
+      description: tool.description,
+      inputSchema: tool.inputSchema,
+    })),
+  }));
+
+  server.setRequestHandler(ListResourcesRequestSchema, async () => ({
+    resources: [],
+  }));
+
+  server.setRequestHandler(ReadResourceRequestSchema, async () => {
+    throw new McpError(ErrorCode.InvalidParams, "Unknown resource URI");
+  });
+
+  server.setRequestHandler(CallToolRequestSchema, async (request, extra) => {
+    const toolName = request.params.name;
+    const args = request.params.arguments ?? {};
+    const reqCtx = createRequestContext({ transport: transportName });
+
+    const rateDecision = rateLimiter.check(sessionKey);
+    if (!rateDecision.allowed) {
+      logger.warn("MCP session rate limit exceeded", {
+        tool: toolName,
+        requestId: reqCtx.requestId,
+        retryAfterSeconds: rateDecision.retryAfterSeconds,
+      });
+      return {
+        isError: true,
+        content: [
+          {
+            type: "text",
+            text: serializeBoundedToolOutput(
+              {
+                ok: false,
+                error: `Rate limit exceeded. Retry after ${rateDecision.retryAfterSeconds}s.`,
+                retryAfterSeconds: rateDecision.retryAfterSeconds,
+              },
+              options.maxToolOutputChars
+            ),
+          },
+        ],
+      };
+    }
+
+    const result = await executeTool(toolName, args, {
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+      requestId: reqCtx.requestId,
+      requestContext: reqCtx,
+      signal: extra?.signal,
+    });
+
+    return {
+      isError: !result.ok,
+      content: [
+        {
+          type: "text",
+          text: serializeBoundedToolOutput(result, options.maxToolOutputChars),
+        },
+      ],
+    };
+  });
+
+  server.onclose = () => {
+    const snapshot = getMetrics();
+    console.error("[metrics]", JSON.stringify(snapshot));
+    if (ownsRateLimiter) {
+      rateLimiter.destroy();
+    }
+    if (ownsCache && cache instanceof MemoryCache) {
+      cache.destroy();
+    }
+  };
+
+  return server;
+}

+/**
+ * Tool Registry with strict Zod schema enforcement before handler execution.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports.
+ */
+
+import type { z } from "zod";
+import type { RequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import type { ApiConfig, Cache, LookupResult } from "../types/index.js";
+
+export interface ToolExecutionContext {
+  apiConfig: ApiConfig;
+  cache: Cache;
+  requestId?: string;
+  requestContext?: RequestContext;
+  signal?: AbortSignal;
+  headers?: Record<string, string>;
+}
+
+export interface RegisteredTool<TArgs = unknown> {
+  name: string;
+  description: string;
+  inputSchema: Record<string, unknown>;
+  schema: z.ZodType<TArgs>;
+  handler: (args: TArgs, context: ToolExecutionContext) => Promise<LookupResult>;
+}
+
+export interface PublicToolDefinition {
+  name: string;
+  description: string;
+  inputSchema: Record<string, unknown>;
+}
+
+export class ToolRegistry {
+  private readonly tools = new Map<string, RegisteredTool<unknown>>();
+
+  register<TArgs>(tool: RegisteredTool<TArgs>): void {
+    this.tools.set(tool.name, tool as RegisteredTool<unknown>);
+  }
+
+  has(name: string): boolean {
+    return this.tools.has(name);
+  }
+
+  get(name: string): RegisteredTool<unknown> | undefined {
+    return this.tools.get(name);
+  }
+
+  list(): PublicToolDefinition[] {
+    return Array.from(this.tools.values()).map(({ name, description, inputSchema }) => ({
+      name,
+      description,
+      inputSchema,
+    }));
+  }
+
+  names(): string[] {
+    return Array.from(this.tools.keys());
+  }
+
+  async execute(
+    name: string,
+    rawArgs: unknown,
+    context: ToolExecutionContext
+  ): Promise<LookupResult> {
+    const tool = this.tools.get(name);
+    if (!tool) {
+      return {
+        ok: false,
+        error: `Unknown tool: ${name}`,
+      };
+    }
+
+    const inputObject =
+      rawArgs === undefined || rawArgs === null ? {} : rawArgs;
+    const parsed = tool.schema.safeParse(inputObject);
+    if (!parsed.success) {
+      const issues = parsed.error.issues
+        .map((issue) => {
+          const loc = issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "input";
+          return `${loc}: ${issue.message}`;
+        })
+        .join("; ");
+      return {
+        ok: false,
+        error: `Validation error for tool '${name}': ${issues}`,
+      };
+    }
+
+    return tool.handler(parsed.data, context);
+  }
+
+  clear(): void {
+    this.tools.clear();
+  }
+}
+
+export const defaultToolRegistry = new ToolRegistry();

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..aa32aa9

+++ b/lib/core/tools.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,150 @@
+/**
+ * Core tool catalog wrapper and RED + cache-hit metrics collector.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports for Cloudflare Workers parity.
+ */
+
+import { logger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import {
+  defaultToolRegistry,
+  type PublicToolDefinition,
+  type ToolExecutionContext,
+} from "../tools/index.js";
+import type { LookupResult, Metrics, ToolMetrics } from "../types/index.js";
+
+export type { PublicToolDefinition, ToolExecutionContext };
+
+export const TOOL_DEFINITIONS: PublicToolDefinition[] = defaultToolRegistry.list();
+
+const globalMetrics: Metrics = {
+  requests: 0,
+  cacheHits: 0,
+  errors: 0,
+  totalTime: 0,
+  startTime: Date.now(),
+};
+
+const perToolMetrics = new Map<string, ToolMetrics>();
+
+function nowMs(): number {
+  if (typeof performance !== "undefined" && typeof performance.now === "function") {
+    return performance.now();
+  }
+  return Date.now();
+}
+
+function recordToolMetrics(
+  toolName: string,
+  durationMs: number,
+  ok: boolean,
+  cached: boolean
+): void {
+  globalMetrics.requests++;
+  globalMetrics.totalTime += durationMs;
+  if (!ok) {
+    globalMetrics.errors++;
+  }
+  if (cached) {
+    globalMetrics.cacheHits++;
+  }
+
+  let entry = perToolMetrics.get(toolName);
+  if (!entry) {
+    entry = {
+      requests: 0,
+      errors: 0,
+      cacheHits: 0,
+      totalTimeMs: 0,
+      avgTimeMs: 0,
+      maxTimeMs: 0,
+    };
+    perToolMetrics.set(toolName, entry);
+  }
+
+  entry.requests++;
+  if (!ok) {
+    entry.errors++;
+  }
+  if (cached) {
+    entry.cacheHits++;
+  }
+  entry.totalTimeMs += durationMs;
+  entry.avgTimeMs = entry.totalTimeMs / entry.requests;
+  if (durationMs > entry.maxTimeMs) {
+    entry.maxTimeMs = durationMs;
+  }
+}
+
+export function hasTool(name: string): boolean {
+  return defaultToolRegistry.has(name);
+}
+
+export function getToolNames(): string[] {
+  return defaultToolRegistry.names();
+}
+
+export async function executeTool(
+  name: string,
+  args: unknown,
+  context: ToolExecutionContext
+): Promise<LookupResult> {
+  const startedAt = nowMs();
+
+  try {
+    const result = await defaultToolRegistry.execute(name, args, context);
+    const durationMs = Math.max(0, nowMs() - startedAt);
+    const isCached =
+      result.ok &&
+      typeof result.data === "object" &&
+      result.data !== null &&
+      (result.data as { cached?: unknown }).cached === true;
+
+    recordToolMetrics(name, durationMs, result.ok, isCached);
+
+    logger.debug("Executed MCP tool", {
+      tool: name,
+      ok: result.ok,
+      cached: isCached,
+      durationMs: Math.round(durationMs * 100) / 100,
+      requestId: context.requestId,
+    });
+
+    return result;
+  } catch (error) {
+    const durationMs = Math.max(0, nowMs() - startedAt);
+    recordToolMetrics(name, durationMs, false, false);
+    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
+    logger.error("Unhandled tool execution error", {
+      tool: name,
+      error: message,
+      requestId: context.requestId,
+    });
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      error: message,
+    };
+  }
+}
+
+export function getMetrics(): Metrics & {
+  uptimeMs: number;
+  tools: Record<string, ToolMetrics>;
+} {
+  const tools: Record<string, ToolMetrics> = {};
+  for (const [name, m] of perToolMetrics.entries()) {
+    tools[name] = { ...m };
+  }
+  return {
+    ...globalMetrics,
+    uptimeMs: Math.max(0, Date.now() - globalMetrics.startTime),
+    tools,
+  };
+}
+
+export function resetMetrics(): void {
+  globalMetrics.requests = 0;
+  globalMetrics.cacheHits = 0;
+  globalMetrics.errors = 0;
+  globalMetrics.totalTime = 0;
+  globalMetrics.startTime = Date.now();
+  perToolMetrics.clear();
+}

+/**
+ * Per-origin Circuit Breaker for upstream public APIs.
+ * Transitions: CLOSED -> OPEN (after failureThreshold failures) -> HALF_OPEN (after resetTimeoutMs) -> CLOSED.
+ */
+
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../../config/timeouts.js";
+import { CIRCUIT_BREAKER } from "../../shared/utils/constants.js";
+
+export type CircuitState = "CLOSED" | "OPEN" | "HALF_OPEN";
+
+export interface CircuitBreakerOptions {
+  service?: string;
+  failureThreshold?: number;
+  resetTimeoutMs?: number;
+  halfOpenMaxAttempts?: number;
+}
+
+export class CircuitBreakerOpenError extends Error {
+  readonly service: string;
+  readonly nextAttemptAt: number;
+
+  constructor(service: string, nextAttemptAt: number) {
+    super(`Circuit breaker is OPEN for '${service}' until ${new Date(nextAttemptAt).toISOString()}`);
+    this.name = "CircuitBreakerOpenError";
+    this.service = service;
+    this.nextAttemptAt = nextAttemptAt;
+  }
+}
+
+export interface CircuitBreakerMetrics {
+  service: string;
+  state: CircuitState;
+  failures: number;
+  successes: number;
+  consecutiveFailures: number;
+  halfOpenAttempts: number;
+  nextAttemptAt: number;
+}
+
+export class CircuitBreaker {
+  private readonly service: string;
+  private readonly failureThreshold: number;
+  private readonly resetTimeoutMs: number;
+  private readonly halfOpenMaxAttempts: number;
+
+  private state: CircuitState = "CLOSED";
+  private failures = 0;
+  private successes = 0;
+  private consecutiveFailures = 0;
+  private halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+  private halfOpenSuccesses = 0;
+  private nextAttemptAt = 0;
+
+  constructor(options: CircuitBreakerOptions = {}) {
+    this.service = options.service ?? "upstream";
+    this.failureThreshold =
+      options.failureThreshold && options.failureThreshold > 0
+        ? options.failureThreshold
+        : CIRCUIT_BREAKER.FAILURE_THRESHOLD;
+    this.resetTimeoutMs =
+      options.resetTimeoutMs && options.resetTimeoutMs > 0
+        ? options.resetTimeoutMs
+        : TIMEOUTS.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS;
+    this.halfOpenMaxAttempts =
+      options.halfOpenMaxAttempts && options.halfOpenMaxAttempts > 0
+        ? options.halfOpenMaxAttempts
+        : CIRCUIT_BREAKER.HALF_OPEN_MAX_ATTEMPTS;
+  }
+
+  getState(now: number = Date.now()): CircuitState {
+    if (this.state === "OPEN" && now >= this.nextAttemptAt) {
+      this.state = "HALF_OPEN";
+      this.halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+      this.halfOpenSuccesses = 0;
+    }
+    return this.state;
+  }
+
+  getMetrics(): CircuitBreakerMetrics {
+    return {
+      service: this.service,
+      state: this.getState(),
+      failures: this.failures,
+      successes: this.successes,
+      consecutiveFailures: this.consecutiveFailures,
+      halfOpenAttempts: this.halfOpenAttempts,
+      nextAttemptAt: this.nextAttemptAt,
+    };
+  }
+
+  async execute<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
+    const current = this.getState();
+
+    if (current === "OPEN") {
+      throw new CircuitBreakerOpenError(this.service, this.nextAttemptAt);
+    }
+
+    if (current === "HALF_OPEN") {
+      if (this.halfOpenAttempts >= this.halfOpenMaxAttempts) {
+        throw new CircuitBreakerOpenError(this.service, this.nextAttemptAt);
+      }
+      this.halfOpenAttempts++;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      const result = await fn();
+      this.onSuccess();
+      return result;
+    } catch (error) {
+      this.onFailure();
+      throw error;
+    }
+  }
+
+  private onSuccess(): void {
+    this.successes++;
+    this.consecutiveFailures = 0;
+
+    if (this.state === "HALF_OPEN") {
+      this.halfOpenSuccesses++;
+      if (this.halfOpenSuccesses >= this.halfOpenMaxAttempts) {
+        this.state = "CLOSED";
+        this.halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+        this.halfOpenSuccesses = 0;
+      }
+    } else {
+      this.state = "CLOSED";
+    }
+  }
+
+  private onFailure(): void {
+    this.failures++;
+    this.consecutiveFailures++;
+
+    if (this.state === "HALF_OPEN" || this.consecutiveFailures >= this.failureThreshold) {
+      this.state = "OPEN";
+      this.nextAttemptAt = Date.now() + this.resetTimeoutMs;
+      this.halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+      this.halfOpenSuccesses = 0;
+    }
+  }
+
+  reset(): void {
+    this.state = "CLOSED";
+    this.consecutiveFailures = 0;
+    this.halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+    this.halfOpenSuccesses = 0;
+    this.nextAttemptAt = 0;
+  }
+}
diff --git a/lib/infrastructure/logger.ts b/lib/infrastructure/logger.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c28df2f
