+/**
+ * OAuth 2.0 metadata & endpoint handlers for MCP remote clients (RFC 8414 / RFC 9728).
+ * Zero hardcoded secrets and zero `node:*` imports.
+ */
+
+import { SERVER } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import { constantTimeEqual } from "./auth.js";
+import type { WorkerEnv } from "./types.js";
+
+function resolveIssuer(request: Request, env: WorkerEnv): string {
+  if (typeof env.OAUTH_ISSUER === "string" && env.OAUTH_ISSUER.trim().length > 0) {
+    return env.OAUTH_ISSUER.trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");
+  }
+  return new URL(request.url).origin;
+}
+
+function getAllowedRedirectUris(env: WorkerEnv): string[] {
+  if (typeof env.OAUTH_REDIRECT_URIS !== "string" || env.OAUTH_REDIRECT_URIS.trim().length === 0) {
+    return [];
+  }
+  return env.OAUTH_REDIRECT_URIS.split(",")
+    .map((u) => u.trim())
+    .filter((u) => u.length > 0);
+}
+
+export function handleOAuthMetadata(
+  request: Request,
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  headers: Record<string, string> = {}
+): Response {
+  const url = new URL(request.url);
+  const issuer = resolveIssuer(request, env);
+
+  if (url.pathname === "/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource") {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        resource: `${issuer}/mcp`,
+        authorization_servers: [issuer],
+        bearer_methods_supported: ["header"],
+        resource_name: SERVER.NAME,
+      }),
+      {
+        status: 200,
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers },
+      }
+    );
+  }
+
+  return new Response(
+    JSON.stringify({
+      issuer,
+      authorization_endpoint: `${issuer}/oauth/authorize`,
+      token_endpoint: `${issuer}/oauth/token`,
+      response_types_supported: ["code"],
+      grant_types_supported: ["authorization_code", "client_credentials"],
+      code_challenge_methods_supported: ["S256"],
+      token_endpoint_auth_methods_supported: ["client_secret_post", "none"],
+      service_documentation: `${issuer}/openapi.json`,
+    }),
+    {
+      status: 200,
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers },
+    }
+  );
+}
+
+export function handleOAuthAuthorize(
+  request: Request,
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  headers: Record<string, string> = {}
+): Response {
+  if (!env.OAUTH_CLIENT_ID) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        error: "unsupported_operation",
+        error_description: "OAuth authorization is not enabled on this deployment",
+      }),
+      { status: 501, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+    );
+  }
+
+  const url = new URL(request.url);
+  const clientId = url.searchParams.get("client_id") ?? "";
+  const redirectUri = url.searchParams.get("redirect_uri") ?? "";
+  const responseType = url.searchParams.get("response_type") ?? "";
+  const codeChallenge = url.searchParams.get("code_challenge") ?? "";
+  const codeChallengeMethod = url.searchParams.get("code_challenge_method") ?? "";
+
+  if (!constantTimeEqual(clientId, env.OAUTH_CLIENT_ID)) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: "unauthorized_client", error_description: "Unknown client_id" }),
+      { status: 400, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+    );
+  }
+
+  const allowedRedirects = getAllowedRedirectUris(env);
+  if (allowedRedirects.length === 0 || !allowedRedirects.includes(redirectUri)) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: "invalid_request", error_description: "Disallowed redirect_uri" }),
+      { status: 400, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (responseType !== "code" || !codeChallenge || codeChallengeMethod !== "S256") {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        error: "invalid_request",
+        error_description: "response_type=code and PKCE S256 code_challenge are required",
+      }),
+      { status: 400, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+    );
+  }
+
+  return new Response(
+    JSON.stringify({
+      error: "unsupported_operation",
+      error_description: "Interactive OAuth consent screen requires external IdP delegation",
+    }),
+    { status: 501, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+  );
+}
+
+export async function handleOAuthToken(
+  request: Request,
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  headers: Record<string, string> = {}
+): Promise<Response> {
+  if (!env.OAUTH_CLIENT_ID || !env.OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        error: "unsupported_operation",
+        error_description: "OAuth token issuance is not configured on this deployment",
+      }),
+      { status: 501, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (request.method !== "POST") {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: "invalid_request", error_description: "POST required" }),
+      { status: 405, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+    );
+  }
+
+  const bodyText = await request.text();
+  const params = new URLSearchParams(bodyText);
+  const grantType = params.get("grant_type") ?? "";
+  const clientId = params.get("client_id") ?? "";
+  const clientSecret = params.get("client_secret") ?? "";
+
+  if (
+    !constantTimeEqual(clientId, env.OAUTH_CLIENT_ID) ||
+    !constantTimeEqual(clientSecret, env.OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET)
+  ) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: "invalid_client", error_description: "Invalid client credentials" }),
+      { status: 401, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (grantType !== "client_credentials" && grantType !== "authorization_code") {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: "unsupported_grant_type" }),
+      { status: 400, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+    );
+  }
+
+  return new Response(
+    JSON.stringify({
+      error: "unsupported_operation",
+      error_description: "Configure a stateless signing key or external IdP to mint OAuth tokens",
+    }),
+    { status: 501, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } }
+  );
+}

+/**
+ * OpenAPI 3.0 specification generator for the Cloudflare Worker REST & MCP endpoints.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports.
+ */
+
+import { TOOL_NAMES } from "../core/tool-schemas.js";
+import { SERVER } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+
+export function buildOpenApiSpec(serverUrl: string = "https://mcp-orcamento.aredes.me") {
+  return {
+    openapi: "3.0.3",
+    info: {
+      title: "MCP Orçamento API",
+      version: SERVER.VERSION,
+      description:
+        "API MCP e REST para consulta de dados orçamentários e fiscais públicos brasileiros (SICONFI, Transferegov e SADIPEM/CKAN do Tesouro Nacional).",
+      license: {
+        name: "AGPL-3.0-only",
+      },
+    },
+    servers: [{ url: serverUrl }],
+    components: {
+      securitySchemes: {
+        BearerAuth: {
+          type: "http",
+          scheme: "bearer",
+        },
+        ApiKeyAuth: {
+          type: "apiKey",
+          in: "header",
+          name: "X-API-Key",
+        },
+      },
+    },
+    paths: {
+      "/health": {
+        get: {
+          summary: "Health check and readiness telemetry",
+          responses: {
+            "200": {
+              description: "Worker health payload",
+            },
+          },
+        },
+      },
+      "/mcp": {
+        post: {
+          summary: "JSON-RPC 2.0 MCP endpoint",
+          security: [{}, { BearerAuth: [] }, { ApiKeyAuth: [] }],
+          requestBody: {
+            required: true,
+            content: {
+              "application/json": {
+                schema: { type: "object" },
+              },
+            },
+          },
+          responses: {
+            "200": { description: "JSON-RPC response" },
+            "400": { description: "Malformed JSON or JSON-RPC payload" },
+            "401": { description: "Authentication required" },
+            "429": { description: "Rate limit exceeded" },
+          },
+        },
+      },
+      "/sse": {
+        get: {
+          summary: "Open Server-Sent Events MCP stream",
+          security: [{}, { BearerAuth: [] }, { ApiKeyAuth: [] }],
+          responses: {
+            "200": { description: "SSE stream" },
+          },
+        },
+        post: {
+          summary: "Deliver JSON-RPC message to SSE session",
+          security: [{}, { BearerAuth: [] }, { ApiKeyAuth: [] }],
+          responses: {
+            "200": { description: "JSON-RPC response" },
+          },
+        },
+      },
+      "/tools/{toolName}": {
+        post: {
+          summary: "Execute a specific MCP Budget/Fiscal tool via REST JSON body",
+          security: [{}, { BearerAuth: [] }, { ApiKeyAuth: [] }],
+          parameters: [
+            {
+              name: "toolName",
+              in: "path",
+              required: true,
+              schema: {
+                type: "string",
+                enum: [...TOOL_NAMES],
+              },
+            },
+          ],
+          requestBody: {
+            required: true,
+            content: {
+              "application/json": {
+                schema: { type: "object" },
+              },
+            },
+          },
+          responses: {
+            "200": { description: "Tool execution result" },
+            "400": { description: "Invalid JSON, encoding, or tool parameters" },
+            "404": { description: "Unknown tool" },
+            "429": { description: "Rate limit exceeded" },
+            "500": { description: "Internal error" },
+          },
+        },
+      },
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+export function handleOpenApiEndpoint(
+  request: Request,
+  extraHeaders: Record<string, string> = {}
+): Response {
+  const origin = new URL(request.url).origin;
+  return new Response(JSON.stringify(buildOpenApiSpec(origin), null, 2), {
+    status: 200,
+    headers: {
+      "Content-Type": "application/json",
+      ...extraHeaders,
+    },
+  });
+}

+/**
+ * Cloudflare Worker rate limiting (KV-backed fixed-window with in-memory fallback).
+ * Avoids global-scope setInterval for Workers runtime compatibility.
+ */
+
+import { RateLimiter } from "../infrastructure/rate-limiter.js";
+import type { WorkerEnv, WorkerRateLimitResult } from "./types.js";
+
+const DEFAULT_WORKER_RATE_LIMIT = 100;
+const WINDOW_SECONDS = 60;
+const MIN_KV_TTL_SECONDS = 60;
+
+// cleanupIntervalMs=0 prevents forbidden global timers inside Cloudflare Workers
+let memoryLimiter = new RateLimiter({
+  maxRequests: DEFAULT_WORKER_RATE_LIMIT,
+  windowMs: WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000,
+  cleanupIntervalMs: 0,
+});
+let configuredLimit = DEFAULT_WORKER_RATE_LIMIT;
+
+export function parseWorkerRateLimit(env: WorkerEnv): number {
+  const raw = env.MCP_RATE_LIMIT;
+  if (typeof raw !== "string" || !/^\d+$/.test(raw.trim())) {
+    return DEFAULT_WORKER_RATE_LIMIT;
+  }
+  const parsed = Number(raw.trim());
+  return Number.isSafeInteger(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : DEFAULT_WORKER_RATE_LIMIT;
+}
+
+export function getClientIdentifier(request: Request): string {
+  const cfIp = request.headers.get("cf-connecting-ip");
+  if (cfIp && cfIp.trim().length > 0) {
+    return cfIp.trim();
+  }
+  const forwardedFor = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for");
+  if (forwardedFor && forwardedFor.trim().length > 0) {
+    return forwardedFor.split(",")[0].trim() || "anonymous";
+  }
+  return "anonymous";
+}
+
+export async function checkWorkerRateLimit(
+  request: Request,
+  env: WorkerEnv
+): Promise<WorkerRateLimitResult> {
+  const limit = parseWorkerRateLimit(env);
+  const clientId = getClientIdentifier(request);
+  const now = Date.now();
+  const windowBucket = Math.floor(now / (WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000));
+  const resetAt = (windowBucket + 1) * WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000;
+  const retryAfterSeconds = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((resetAt - now) / 1000));
+
+  const kv = env.MCP_KV ?? env.MCP_CACHE;
+  if (kv) {
+    const key = `rl:${clientId}:${windowBucket}`;
+    try {
+      const existing = await kv.get(key, "json");
+      const currentCount =
+        typeof existing === "object" &&
+        existing !== null &&
+        typeof (existing as { count?: unknown }).count === "number"
+          ? ((existing as { count: number }).count as number)
+          : 0;
+
+      if (currentCount >= limit) {
+        return {
+          allowed: false,
+          limit,
+          remaining: 0,
+          resetAt,
+          retryAfterSeconds,
+          backend: "kv",
+        };
+      }
+
+      const nextCount = currentCount + 1;
+      await kv.put(key, JSON.stringify({ count: nextCount }), {
+        expirationTtl: MIN_KV_TTL_SECONDS,
+      });
+
+      return {
+        allowed: true,
+        limit,
+        remaining: Math.max(0, limit - nextCount),
+        resetAt,
+        retryAfterSeconds: 0,
+        backend: "kv",
+      };
+    } catch {
+      // Fall back to in-memory limiter if KV experiences a transient error
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (limit !== configuredLimit) {
+    configuredLimit = limit;
+    memoryLimiter = new RateLimiter({
+      maxRequests: limit,
+      windowMs: WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000,
+      cleanupIntervalMs: 0,
+    });
+  }
+
+  memoryLimiter.cleanup(now);
+  const decision = memoryLimiter.check(clientId, now);
+  return {
+    allowed: decision.allowed,
+    limit: decision.limit,
+    remaining: decision.remaining,
+    resetAt: decision.resetAt,
+    retryAfterSeconds: decision.retryAfterSeconds,
+    backend: "memory",
+  };
+}
+
+export function rateLimitHeaders(result: WorkerRateLimitResult): Record<string, string> {
+  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
+    "X-RateLimit-Limit": String(result.limit),
+    "X-RateLimit-Remaining": String(result.remaining),
+    "X-RateLimit-Reset": String(Math.ceil(result.resetAt / 1000)),
+  };
+
+  if (!result.allowed) {
+    headers["Retry-After"] = String(result.retryAfterSeconds);
+  }
+
+  return headers;
+}
+
+export function resetWorkerMemoryRateLimiter(): void {
+  memoryLimiter.reset();
+}

+/**
+ * Generic REST adapter for POST /tools/{toolName} inside Cloudflare Worker.
+ * Enforces tool allowlist, UTF-8 / JSON object parsing, and forwards to handleMCPRequest.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports.
+ */
+
+import {
+  getCorsHeaders,
+  handleMCPRequest,
+  readBoundedJsonBody,
+} from "../adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { hasTool } from "../core/tools.js";
+import { logger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import {
+  createRequestContext,
+  REQUEST_ID_HEADER,
+  type RequestContext,
+} from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import type { MCPRequest } from "../types/index.js";
+import type { WorkerEnv } from "./types.js";
+
+export async function handleToolRestEndpoint(
+  toolName: string,
+  request: Request,
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext
+): Promise<Response> {
+  const reqCtx =
+    requestContext ?? createRequestContext({ headers: request.headers, transport: "worker-rest" });
+  const corsHeaders = getCorsHeaders(request, env);
+  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
+    "Content-Type": "application/json",
+    [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+    ...corsHeaders,
+  };
+
+  if (!hasTool(toolName)) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        ok: false,
+        error: `Unknown tool: ${toolName}`,
+      }),
+      { status: 404, headers }
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (request.method !== "POST") {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        ok: false,
+        error: "Method not allowed; use POST",
+      }),
+      { status: 405, headers }
+    );
+  }
+
+  let parsedArgs: Record<string, unknown>;
+  try {
+    const parsed = await readBoundedJsonBody(request);
+    if (!parsed.ok) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          ok: false,
+          error: parsed.error,
+        }),
+        { status: parsed.status, headers }
+      );
+    }
+
+    if (typeof parsed.body !== "object" || parsed.body === null || Array.isArray(parsed.body)) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          ok: false,
+          error: "REST tool body must be a JSON object",
+        }),
+        { status: 400, headers }
+      );
+    }
+    parsedArgs = parsed.body as Record<string, unknown>;
+  } catch (error) {
+    logger.error("Failed reading REST tool body", {
+      tool: toolName,
+      requestId: reqCtx.requestId,
+      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+    });
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        ok: false,
+        error: "Failed to read request body",
+      }),
+      { status: 400, headers }
+    );
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const mcpRequest: MCPRequest = {
+      jsonrpc: "2.0",
+      id: reqCtx.requestId,
+      method: "tools/call",
+      params: {
+        name: toolName,
+        arguments: parsedArgs,
+      },
+    };
+
+    const mcpResponse = await handleMCPRequest(mcpRequest, env, reqCtx);
+    if (mcpResponse.error) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          ok: false,
+          error: mcpResponse.error.message,
+          code: mcpResponse.error.code,
+        }),
+        { status: 500, headers }
+      );
+    }
+
+    const resultPayload = mcpResponse.result as {
+      isError?: boolean;
+      content?: Array<{ type: string; text?: string }>;
+    };
+    const text = resultPayload?.content?.[0]?.text ?? "{}";
+    const parsedOutput = JSON.parse(text) as { ok?: boolean; error?: string };
+
+    if (resultPayload?.isError || parsedOutput?.ok === false) {
+      const errorText = String(parsedOutput?.error ?? "");
+      const status = errorText.toLowerCase().includes("validation error") ? 400 : 502;
+      return new Response(text, { status, headers });
+    }
+
+    return new Response(text, { status: 200, headers });
+  } catch (error) {
+    logger.error("REST tool execution failed", {
+      tool: toolName,
+      requestId: reqCtx.requestId,
+      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+    });
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        ok: false,
+        error: "Internal server error while executing tool",
+      }),
+      { status: 500, headers }
+    );
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Cloudflare Worker SSE transport handler with optional KV session persistence.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports.
+ */
+
+import {
+  getCorsHeaders,
+  handleMCPRequest,
+  readBoundedJsonBody,
+} from "../adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import {
+  createRequestContext,
+  generateRequestId,
+  REQUEST_ID_HEADER,
+  type RequestContext,
+} from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { SERVER } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import type { MCPRequest } from "../types/index.js";
+import type { SSESessionRecord, WorkerEnv } from "./types.js";
+
+const SSE_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS = 300;
+const FALLBACK_SESSIONS = new Map<string, SSESessionRecord>();
+
+export async function createSSESession(
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  sessionId: string = generateRequestId()
+): Promise<SSESessionRecord> {
+  const now = Date.now();
+  const record: SSESessionRecord = {
+    sessionId,
+    createdAt: now,
+    lastActivityAt: now,
+  };
+
+  const kv = env.MCP_KV ?? env.MCP_CACHE;
+  if (kv) {
+    try {
+      await kv.put(`sse:session:${sessionId}`, JSON.stringify(record), {
+        expirationTtl: SSE_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS,
+      });
+      return record;
+    } catch {
+      // Fallback to in-memory map on KV failure
+    }
+  }
+
+  FALLBACK_SESSIONS.set(sessionId, record);
+  return record;
+}
+
+export async function getSSESession(
+  sessionId: string,
+  env: WorkerEnv
+): Promise<SSESessionRecord | null> {
+  const kv = env.MCP_KV ?? env.MCP_CACHE;
+  if (kv) {
+    try {
+      const value = await kv.get(`sse:session:${sessionId}`, "json");
+      if (value && typeof value === "object") {
+        return value as SSESessionRecord;
+      }
+    } catch {
+      // Fallback
+    }
+  }
+  return FALLBACK_SESSIONS.get(sessionId) ?? null;
+}
+
+export function resetSSESessions(): void {
+  FALLBACK_SESSIONS.clear();
+}
+
+export async function handleSSEEndpoint(
+  request: Request,
+  env: WorkerEnv,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext
+): Promise<Response> {
+  const reqCtx =
+    requestContext ?? createRequestContext({ headers: request.headers, transport: "worker-sse" });
+  const corsHeaders = getCorsHeaders(request, env);
+  const url = new URL(request.url);
+
+  if (request.method === "GET") {
+    const session = await createSSESession(env);
+    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+
+    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
+      start(controller) {
+        const endpointEvent = `event: endpoint\ndata: /sse?sessionId=${session.sessionId}\n\n`;
+        const connectedEvent = `event: connected\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({
+          server: SERVER.NAME,
+          version: SERVER.VERSION,
+          sessionId: session.sessionId,
+        })}\n\n`;
+
+        controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(endpointEvent));
+        controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(connectedEvent));
+        controller.close();
+      },
+    });
+
+    return new Response(stream, {
+      status: 200,
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "text/event-stream; charset=utf-8",
+        "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
+        Connection: "keep-alive",
+        [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+        ...corsHeaders,
+      },
+    });
+  }
+
+  if (request.method === "POST") {
+    const sessionId = url.searchParams.get("sessionId");
+    if (sessionId) {
+      const existing = await getSSESession(sessionId, env);
+      if (existing) {
+        existing.lastActivityAt = Date.now();
+        const kv = env.MCP_KV ?? env.MCP_CACHE;
+        if (kv) {
+          try {
+            await kv.put(`sse:session:${sessionId}`, JSON.stringify(existing), {
+              expirationTtl: SSE_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS,
+            });
+          } catch {
+            // Non-fatal
+          }
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const parsed = await readBoundedJsonBody(request);
+    if (!parsed.ok) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id: null,
+          error: { code: parsed.code, message: parsed.error },
+        }),
+        {
+          status: parsed.status,
+          headers: {
+            "Content-Type": "application/json",
+            [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+            ...corsHeaders,
+          },
+        }
+      );
+    }
+
+    const rpcResponse = await handleMCPRequest(parsed.body as MCPRequest, env, reqCtx);
+    return new Response(JSON.stringify(rpcResponse), {
+      status: 200,
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+        ...corsHeaders,
+      },
+    });
+  }
+
+  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Method Not Allowed" }), {
+    status: 405,
+    headers: {
+      "Content-Type": "application/json",
+      [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+      ...corsHeaders,
+    },
+  });
+}

+/**
+ * Cloudflare Worker HTTP fetch entrypoint routing /, /health, /mcp, /sse, /openapi.json,
+ * OAuth metadata/endpoints, and /tools/{toolName}.
+ * Zero `node:*` imports.
+ */
+
+import {
+  getCorsHeaders,
+  handleHealthCheck,
+  handleMCPEndpoint,
+  handleOptions,
+} from "../adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "../core/tools.js";
+import { logger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import {
+  createRequestContext,
+  REQUEST_ID_HEADER,
+} from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { SERVER } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import {
+  authenticateWorkerRequest,
+  createUnauthorizedResponse,
+} from "./auth.js";
+import { handleOpenApiEndpoint } from "./openapi.js";
+import {
+  handleOAuthAuthorize,
+  handleOAuthMetadata,
+  handleOAuthToken,
+} from "./oauth.js";
+import {
+  checkWorkerRateLimit,
+  rateLimitHeaders,
+} from "./rate-limit.js";
+import { handleToolRestEndpoint } from "./rest.js";
+import { handleSSEEndpoint } from "./sse.js";
+import type { WorkerEnv } from "./types.js";
+
+const SECURITY_HEADERS: Record<string, string> = {
+  "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
+  "X-Frame-Options": "DENY",
+  "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer",
+  "Cache-Control": "no-store",
+};
+
+function attachResponseHeaders(
+  response: Response,
+  headersToAdd: Record<string, string>
+): Response {
+  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
+  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(headersToAdd)) {
+    if (!headers.has(k)) {
+      headers.set(k, v);
+    }
+  }
+  return new Response(response.body, {
+    status: response.status,
+    statusText: response.statusText,
+    headers,
+  });
+}
+
+export async function handleWorkerFetch(
+  request: Request,
+  env: WorkerEnv
+): Promise<Response> {
+  const reqCtx = createRequestContext({
+    headers: request.headers,
+    transport: "cloudflare-worker",
+  });
+  const corsHeaders = getCorsHeaders(request, env);
+  const commonHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
+    [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+    ...SECURITY_HEADERS,
+    ...corsHeaders,
+  };
+
+  try {
+    const url = new URL(request.url);
+    const pathname = url.pathname;
+
+    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
+      return attachResponseHeaders(handleOptions(request, env), {
+        [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+        ...SECURITY_HEADERS,
+      });
+    }
+
+    if (pathname === "/" && request.method === "GET") {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          service: SERVER.NAME,
+          version: SERVER.VERSION,
+          status: "ok",
+          runtime: "cloudflare-workers",
+          tool_count: TOOL_DEFINITIONS.length,
+          tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS.map((t) => t.name),
+          endpoints: {
+            health: "/health",
+            mcp: "/mcp",
+            sse: "/sse",
+            openapi: "/openapi.json",
+            rest_tools: "/tools/{toolName}",
+          },
+        }),
+        {
+          status: 200,
+          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...commonHeaders },
+        }
+      );
+    }
+
+    if (pathname === "/health" && request.method === "GET") {
+      const resp = await handleHealthCheck(env, request, reqCtx);
+      return attachResponseHeaders(resp, SECURITY_HEADERS);
+    }
+
+    if (pathname === "/openapi.json" && request.method === "GET") {
+      return handleOpenApiEndpoint(request, commonHeaders);
+    }
+
+    if (
+      (pathname === "/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server" ||
+        pathname === "/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource") &&
+      request.method === "GET"
+    ) {
+      return handleOAuthMetadata(request, env, commonHeaders);
+    }
+
+    if (pathname === "/oauth/authorize" && request.method === "GET") {
+      return handleOAuthAuthorize(request, env, commonHeaders);
+    }
+
+    if (pathname === "/oauth/token") {
+      return await handleOAuthToken(request, env, commonHeaders);
+    }
+
+    // Protected API endpoints: /mcp, /sse, /tools/:toolName
+    const authResult = authenticateWorkerRequest(request, env);
+    if (!authResult.authenticated) {
+      return createUnauthorizedResponse(authResult, commonHeaders);
+    }
+
+    const rateDecision = await checkWorkerRateLimit(request, env);
+    const rlHeaders = rateLimitHeaders(rateDecision);
+    if (!rateDecision.allowed) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          ok: false,
+          error: "Rate limit exceeded",
+          retryAfterSeconds: rateDecision.retryAfterSeconds,
+        }),
+        {
+          status: 429,
+          headers: {
+            "Content-Type": "application/json",
+            ...commonHeaders,
+            ...rlHeaders,
+          },
+        }
+      );
+    }
+
+    const protectedHeaders = {
+      ...SECURITY_HEADERS,
+      ...rlHeaders,
+      [REQUEST_ID_HEADER]: reqCtx.requestId,
+    };
+
+    if (pathname === "/mcp") {
+      const resp = await handleMCPEndpoint(request, env, reqCtx);
+      return attachResponseHeaders(resp, protectedHeaders);
+    }
+
+    if (pathname === "/sse") {
+      const resp = await handleSSEEndpoint(request, env, reqCtx);
+      return attachResponseHeaders(resp, protectedHeaders);
+    }
+
+    if (pathname.startsWith("/tools/")) {
+      let toolName: string;
+      try {
+        toolName = decodeURIComponent(pathname.slice("/tools/".length));
+      } catch {
+        return new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: "Invalid URL encoding in tool route" }),
+          {
+            status: 400,
+            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...commonHeaders, ...rlHeaders },
+          }
+        );
+      }
+      if (!toolName || toolName.includes("/")) {
+        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: "Invalid tool route" }), {
+          status: 404,
+          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...commonHeaders, ...rlHeaders },
+        });
+      }
+      const resp = await handleToolRestEndpoint(toolName, request, env, reqCtx);
+      return attachResponseHeaders(resp, protectedHeaders);
+    }
+
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: "Not Found", path: pathname }),
+      {
+        status: 404,
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...commonHeaders },
+      }
+    );
+  } catch (error) {
+    logger.error("Unhandled Worker fetch error", {
+      requestId: reqCtx.requestId,
+      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+    });
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: "Internal Server Error" }),
+      {
+        status: 500,
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...commonHeaders },
+      }
+    );
+  }
+}
+
+export default {
+  fetch: handleWorkerFetch,
+};

+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
+import net from "node:net";
+import path from "node:path";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

+const BIN_PATH = path.join(REPO_ROOT, "build/lib/bin/mcp-orcamento.js");
+const CLI_PATH = path.join(REPO_ROOT, "build/lib/adapters/cli.js");

+  "siconfi_entes",
+  "siconfi_extrato_entregas",
+  "siconfi_rreo",

+  "siconfi_dca",

+function getFreePort() {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const srv = net.createServer();
+    srv.unref();
+    srv.on("error", reject);
+    srv.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", () => {
+      const addr = srv.address();
+      const port = typeof addr === "object" && addr ? addr.port : 0;
+      srv.close(() => resolve(port));
+    });
+  });
+}
+
+async function waitForHealth(url, timeoutMs = 8000) {
+  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
+  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
+    try {
+      const resp = await fetch(url);
+      if (resp.ok) {
+        return await resp.json();
+      }
+    } catch {
+      // retry until server listens
+    }
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+  }
+  throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${url}`);
+}
+
+async function testStdioTransport() {
+  const transportErrors = [];
+  const stderrChunks = [];
+
+  const transport = new StdioClientTransport({
+    command: process.execPath,
+    args: [BIN_PATH],
+    cwd: REPO_ROOT,
+    env: {
+      ...process.env,
+      NODE_ENV: "test",
+      TESOURO_ORDS_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:19991/ords/",
+      TRANSFEREGOV_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:19992/",
+      TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:19993/ckan/api/3/action/",
+    },
+    stderr: "pipe",
+  });
+
+  if (transport.stderr) {
+    transport.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stderrChunks.push(chunk.toString("utf8"));
+    });
+  }
+  transport.onerror = (err) => {
+    transportErrors.push(err);
+  };
+
+  const client = new Client(
+    { name: "transport-integration-stdio", version: "0.1.0" },
+    { capabilities: {} }
+  );
+
+  await client.connect(transport);
+
+  const toolsList = await client.listTools();
+  assert.equal(toolsList.tools.length, 8);
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    toolsList.tools.map((t) => t.name),
+    EXPECTED_TOOLS
+  );
+
+  const resourcesList = await client.listResources();
+  assert.deepEqual(resourcesList.resources, []);
+
+  await assert.rejects(
+    client.readResource({ uri: "orcamento://nonexistent" }),
+    /Unknown resource URI/
+  );
+
+  // 101 offline invalid siconfi_entes({}) calls -> all must return isError: true cleanly
+  for (let i = 1; i <= 101; i++) {
+    const callRes = await client.callTool({ name: "siconfi_entes", arguments: {} });
+    assert.equal(callRes.isError, true, `Expected call #${i} to return isError=true`);
+  }
+
+  await client.close();
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
+
+  assert.equal(transportErrors.length, 0, `Unexpected stdio transport errors: ${transportErrors}`);
+  const stderrText = stderrChunks.join("");
+  assert.ok(stderrText.includes("[metrics]"), `Expected [metrics] in stderr, got: ${stderrText}`);
+  console.log("STDIO_TRANSPORT_OK (8 tools, 101 offline calls, [metrics] verified)");
+}
+
+async function testHttpAndSseTransport() {
+  const port = await getFreePort();
+  const baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
+  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [CLI_PATH, "--http", "--port", String(port)], {
+    cwd: REPO_ROOT,
+    env: {
+      ...process.env,
+      NODE_ENV: "test",
+      MCP_HTTP_HOST: "127.0.0.1",
+      TESOURO_ORDS_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:19991/ords/",
+      TRANSFEREGOV_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:19992/",
+      TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:19993/ckan/api/3/action/",
+    },
+    stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
+  });
+
+  try {
+    const health = await waitForHealth(`${baseUrl}/health`);
+    assert.equal(health.status, "healthy");
+    assert.equal(health.service, "mcp-orcamento");
+    assert.equal(health.tools, 8);
+
+    // 1) Streamable HTTP (/mcp)
+    const streamClient = new Client(
+      { name: "transport-integration-streamable", version: "0.1.0" },
+      { capabilities: {} }
+    );
+    const streamTransport = new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(new URL(`${baseUrl}/mcp`));
+    await streamClient.connect(streamTransport);
+    const streamTools = await streamClient.listTools();
+    assert.equal(streamTools.tools.length, 8);
+    const streamResources = await streamClient.listResources();
+    assert.deepEqual(streamResources.resources, []);
+    await assert.rejects(
+      streamClient.readResource({ uri: "orcamento://nonexistent" }),
+      /Unknown resource URI/
+    );
+    const errCall = await streamClient.callTool({ name: "siconfi_entes", arguments: {} });
+    assert.equal(errCall.isError, true);
+    await streamClient.close();
+
+    // 2) Legacy SSE (/sse + /messages)
+    const sseClient = new Client(
+      { name: "transport-integration-sse", version: "0.1.0" },
+      { capabilities: {} }
+    );
+    const sseTransport = new SSEClientTransport(new URL(`${baseUrl}/sse`));
+    await sseClient.connect(sseTransport);
+    const sseTools = await sseClient.listTools();
+    assert.equal(sseTools.tools.length, 8);
+    await sseClient.close();
+
+    console.log("HTTP_SSE_TRANSPORT_OK (StreamableHTTP /mcp + SSE /sse verified)");
+  } finally {
+    child.kill("SIGTERM");
+    await new Promise((resolve) => {
+      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+        child.kill("SIGKILL");
+        resolve();
+      }, 2000);
+      child.once("exit", () => {
+        clearTimeout(timer);
+        resolve();
+      });
+    });
+  }
+}
+
+await testStdioTransport();
+await testHttpAndSseTransport();

+import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
+import path from "node:path";
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { handleHealthCheck } from "../../lib/adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "../../lib/core/tools.js";
+import { TOOL_NAMES } from "../../lib/core/tool-schemas.js";
+import { SERVER } from "../../lib/shared/utils/constants.js";
+import { buildOpenApiSpec } from "../../lib/workers/openapi.js";
+import { handleWorkerFetch } from "../../lib/workers/worker.js";
+
+const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../..");
+
+function collectTsFiles(dir: string): string[] {
+  if (!existsSync(dir)) return [];
+  const files: string[] = [];
+  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
+    const full = path.join(dir, entry);
+    const st = statSync(full);
+    if (st.isDirectory()) {
+      files.push(...collectTsFiles(full));
+    } else if (entry.endsWith(".ts")) {
+      files.push(full);
+    }
+  }
+  return files;
+}
+
+describe("Architecture Hygiene", () => {
+  it("keeps shared, tools, core, workers, and cloudflare adapter free of node:* imports", () => {
+    const targets = [
+      ...collectTsFiles(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "lib/shared")),
+      ...collectTsFiles(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "lib/tools")),
+      ...collectTsFiles(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "lib/core")),
+      ...collectTsFiles(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "lib/workers")),
+      path.join(REPO_ROOT, "lib/adapters/cloudflare.ts"),
+    ];
+
+    const nodeBuiltinPattern = /from\s+["'](?:node:[^"']+|fs|path|os|http|https|crypto|stream|net)["']/g;
+    for (const filePath of targets) {
+      const content = readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
+      const matches = content.match(nodeBuiltinPattern) ?? [];
+      expect(matches, `Unexpected Node import in ${filePath}`).toEqual([]);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("enforces acyclic core -> tools layering (only lib/core/tools.ts imports lib/tools/)", () => {
+    const coreFiles = collectTsFiles(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "lib/core"));
+    for (const filePath of coreFiles) {
+      const content = readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
+      if (path.basename(filePath) === "tools.ts") {
+        expect(content).toContain("../tools/index.js");
+      } else {
+        expect(content).not.toMatch(/from\s+["']\.\.\/tools/);
+      }
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("keeps lib/core/mcp-server.ts concise (<= 180 lines) and free of node:fs", () => {
+    const mcpServerPath = path.join(REPO_ROOT, "lib/core/mcp-server.ts");
+    const content = readFileSync(mcpServerPath, "utf8");
+    const lineCount = content.split("\n").length - (content.endsWith("\n") ? 1 : 0);
+    expect(lineCount).toBeLessThanOrEqual(180);
+    expect(content).not.toContain("node:fs");
+  });
+
+  it("keeps version and 8-tool catalog aligned across package.json, SERVER, OpenAPI, /health, and /", async () => {
+    const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "package.json"), "utf8")) as {
+      name: string;
+      version: string;
+    };
+
+    expect(pkg.name).toBe("@aredes.me/mcp-orcamento");
+    expect(pkg.version).toBe("0.1.0");
+    expect(SERVER.NAME).toBe("mcp-orcamento");
+    expect(SERVER.VERSION).toBe(pkg.version);
+    expect(TOOL_DEFINITIONS).toHaveLength(8);
+    expect(TOOL_NAMES).toHaveLength(8);
+
+    const openApi = buildOpenApiSpec();
+    expect(openApi.info.version).toBe(pkg.version);
+    const enumTools = openApi.paths["/tools/{toolName}"].post.parameters[0].schema.enum;
+    expect(enumTools).toEqual([...TOOL_NAMES]);
+
+    const healthResp = await handleHealthCheck({});
+    const healthJson = (await healthResp.json()) as {
+      service: string;
+      version: string;
+      tools: number;
+    };
+    expect(healthJson.service).toBe("mcp-orcamento");
+    expect(healthJson.version).toBe(pkg.version);
+    expect(healthJson.tools).toBe(8);
+
+    const rootResp = await handleWorkerFetch(new Request("https://example.test/"), {});
+    const rootJson = (await rootResp.json()) as {
+      service: string;
+      version: string;
+      tool_count: number;
+      tools: string[];
+    };
+    expect(rootJson.service).toBe("mcp-orcamento");
+    expect(rootJson.version).toBe(pkg.version);
+    expect(rootJson.tool_count).toBe(8);
+    expect(rootJson.tools).toEqual(TOOL_DEFINITIONS.map((t) => t.name));
+  });
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import { KVCache, MemoryCache, MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS } from "../../lib/core/cache.js";
+import { RateLimiter } from "../../lib/infrastructure/rate-limiter.js";
+
+describe("Cache & Rate Limiter", () => {
+  const caches: MemoryCache[] = [];
+  const limiters: RateLimiter[] = [];
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    while (caches.length > 0) {
+      caches.pop()!.destroy();
+    }
+    while (limiters.length > 0) {
+      limiters.pop()!.dispose();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("MemoryCache performs LRU eviction, TTL expiration, and manual cleanup", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(2, 30, 0);
+    caches.push(cache);
+
+    cache.set("k1", { v: 1 });
+    cache.set("k2", { v: 2 });
+    // Access k1 to promote it as most-recently-used so k2 gets evicted next
+    expect(cache.get("k1")).toEqual({ v: 1 });
+
+    cache.set("k3", { v: 3 });
+    expect(cache.size()).toBe(2);
+    expect(cache.get("k2")).toBeNull();
+    expect(cache.get("k1")).toEqual({ v: 1 });
+    expect(cache.get("k3")).toEqual({ v: 3 });
+
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 40));
+    expect(cache.get("k1")).toBeNull();
+    expect(cache.cleanup()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
+    cache.clear();
+    expect(cache.size()).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it("KVCache enforces minimum 60s expirationTtl and degrades gracefully on KV failures", async () => {
+    const putMock = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+    const getMock = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ cached: true });
+
+    const kv = new KVCache({ get: getMock, put: putMock }, 5_000);
+    await kv.set("item:1", { hello: "world" });
+    expect(putMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith("item:1", JSON.stringify({ hello: "world" }), {
+      expirationTtl: MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS,
+    });
+    expect(await kv.get("item:1")).toEqual({ cached: true });
+
+    const failingKv = new KVCache(
+      {
+        get: vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error("kv down")),
+        put: vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error("kv write fail")),
+      },
+      120_000
+    );
+    await expect(failingKv.get("x")).resolves.toBeNull();
+    await expect(failingKv.set("x", 1)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
+    await expect(failingKv.clear()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it("RateLimiter enforces sliding window limits, maxBuckets eviction, cleanup, and reset", () => {
+    const limiter = new RateLimiter({
+      maxRequests: 2,
+      windowMs: 1_000,
+      cleanupIntervalMs: 0,
+      maxBuckets: 2,
+    });
+    limiters.push(limiter);
+
+    const t0 = 10_000;
+    expect(limiter.isAllowed("ip-1", t0)).toBe(true);
+    expect(limiter.isAllowed("ip-1", t0 + 10)).toBe(true);
+    const denied = limiter.check("ip-1", t0 + 20);
+    expect(denied.allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(denied.retryAfterSeconds).toBe(1);
+
+    // Advances past sliding window
+    expect(limiter.isAllowed("ip-1", t0 + 1_100)).toBe(true);
+
+    // Max buckets = 2 evicts oldest key when a 3rd client arrives
+    limiter.isAllowed("ip-2", t0 + 1_100);
+    limiter.isAllowed("ip-3", t0 + 1_100);
+    expect(limiter.size()).toBe(2);
+
+    expect(limiter.cleanup(t0 + 5_000)).toBe(2);
+    limiter.reset();
+    expect(limiter.size()).toBe(0);
+  });
+});

+import http from "node:http";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { parseCliArgs } from "../../lib/adapters/cli.js";
+import { createHttpApp, startHttpServer, type RunningHttpServer } from "../../lib/adapters/cli-http.js";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../../lib/core/cache.js";
+import type { ApiConfig, ServerConfig } from "../../lib/types/index.js";
+
+const API_CONFIG: ApiConfig = {
+  tesouroOrdsBaseUrl: "https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/",
+  transferegovBaseUrl: "https://api.transferegov.gestao.gov.br/",
+  tesouroCkanBaseUrl: "https://www.tesourotransparente.gov.br/ckan/api/3/action/",
+};
+
+const BASE_SERVER_CONFIG: ServerConfig = {
+  transport: "http",
+  httpPort: 0,
+  httpHost: "127.0.0.1",
+  cacheSize: 32,
+  cacheTTL: 60_000,
+  apiTimeout: 5_000,
+};
+
+function rawHttpRequest(options: {
+  port: number;
+  path: string;
+  method?: string;
+  headers?: Record<string, string>;
+  body?: string;
+}): Promise<{ status: number; headers: http.IncomingHttpHeaders; body: string }> {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const req = http.request(
+      {
+        host: "127.0.0.1",
+        port: options.port,
+        path: options.path,
+        method: options.method ?? "GET",
+        headers: options.headers,
+      },
+      (res) => {
+        const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
+        res.on("data", (c) => chunks.push(Buffer.from(c)));
+        res.on("end", () => {
+          resolve({
+            status: res.statusCode ?? 0,
+            headers: res.headers,
+            body: Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"),
+          });
+        });
+      }
+    );
+    req.on("error", reject);
+    if (options.body !== undefined) {
+      req.write(options.body);
+    }
+    req.end();
+  });
+}
+
+describe("CLI HTTP Server Security & Transports", () => {
+  const servers: RunningHttpServer[] = [];
+  const caches: MemoryCache[] = [];
+
+  afterEach(async () => {
+    while (servers.length > 0) {
+      await servers.pop()!.close();
+    }
+    while (caches.length > 0) {
+      caches.pop()!.destroy();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("parses CLI flags safely", () => {
+    expect(parseCliArgs(["--http", "--port", "4000", "--host=127.0.0.1"])).toEqual({
+      help: false,
+      version: false,
+      transport: "http",
+      port: 4000,
+      host: "127.0.0.1",
+    });
+    expect(parseCliArgs(["--help", "--version", "--port=8080", "--host", "::1"])).toEqual({
+      help: true,
+      version: true,
+      port: 8080,
+      host: "::1",
+    });
+  });
+
+  it("enforces Host DNS rebinding protection, CORS allowlist, Auth, security headers, and rate limits", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(32, 60_000, 0);
+    caches.push(cache);
+
+    const running = await startHttpServer({
+      apiConfig: API_CONFIG,
+      serverConfig: BASE_SERVER_CONFIG,
+      cache,
+      apiKey: "cli-sec-key",
+      rateLimitMax: 4,
+    });
+    servers.push(running);
+
+    // Public /health with security headers
+    const health = await rawHttpRequest({ port: running.port, path: "/health" });
+    expect(health.status).toBe(200);
+    expect(health.headers["x-content-type-options"]).toBe("nosniff");
+    expect(health.headers["x-frame-options"]).toBe("DENY");
+    expect(health.headers["content-security-policy"]).toBe("default-src 'none'");
+    expect(JSON.parse(health.body).service).toBe("mcp-orcamento");
+
+    // DNS rebinding Host rejection -> 403
+    const rebind = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/health",
+      headers: { Host: "evil.attacker.com:3000" },
+    });
+    expect(rebind.status).toBe(403);
+
+    // Disallowed CORS origin -> 403
+    const badCors = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/health",
+      headers: { Origin: "https://evil.example.net" },
+    });
+    expect(badCors.status).toBe(403);
+
+    // Allowed localhost CORS preflight -> 204
+    const okPreflight = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "OPTIONS",
+      headers: { Origin: "http://localhost:5173" },
+    });
+    expect(okPreflight.status).toBe(204);
+    expect(okPreflight.headers["access-control-allow-origin"]).toBe("http://localhost:5173");
+
+    // Auth enforcement on /mcp: 401 missing, 403 wrong, 200 valid
+    const unauth = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 1, method: "tools/list" }),
+    });
+    expect(unauth.status).toBe(401);
+
+    const wrongAuth = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Authorization: "Bearer wrong",
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 1, method: "tools/list" }),
+    });
+    expect(wrongAuth.status).toBe(403);
+
+    const validMcp = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        "X-API-Key": "cli-sec-key",
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 1, method: "tools/list" }),
+    });
+    expect(validMcp.status).toBe(200);
+    expect(JSON.parse(validMcp.body).result.tools).toHaveLength(8);
+
+    // Malformed JSON -> 400
+    const badJson = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Authorization: "Bearer cli-sec-key",
+      },
+      body: "{bad-json",
+    });
+    expect(badJson.status).toBe(400);
+
+    // Oversized body (> 100kb) -> 413
+    const hugeBody = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Authorization: "Bearer cli-sec-key",
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ data: "x".repeat(110_000) }),
+    });
+    expect(hugeBody.status).toBe(413);
+
+    // Rate limit exhaustion (4 max requests -> 5th returns 429)
+    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+      await rawHttpRequest({
+        port: running.port,
+        path: "/mcp",
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: {
+          "Content-Type": "application/json",
+          Authorization: "Bearer cli-sec-key",
+        },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: i + 10, method: "ping" }),
+      });
+    }
+    const limited = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Authorization: "Bearer cli-sec-key",
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 99, method: "ping" }),
+    });
+    expect(limited.status).toBe(429);
+  });
+
+  it("handles Streamable HTTP initialization, SSE /messages validation, and explicit Host/Origin rules", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(16, 60_000, 0);
+    caches.push(cache);
+
+    const running = await startHttpServer({
+      apiConfig: API_CONFIG,
+      serverConfig: BASE_SERVER_CONFIG,
+      cache,
+      allowedHosts: ["127.0.0.1"],
+      allowedOrigins: ["http://127.0.0.1:5173"],
+      rateLimitMax: 20,
+    });
+    servers.push(running);
+
+    // Streamable HTTP initialize session -> returns Mcp-Session-Id
+    const initResp = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}`,
+        Origin: "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Accept: "application/json, text/event-stream",
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: 1,
+        method: "initialize",
+        params: {
+          protocolVersion: "2025-03-26",
+          capabilities: {},
+          clientInfo: { name: "test", version: "1.0" },
+        },
+      }),
+    });
+    expect(initResp.status).toBe(200);
+    const sid = String(initResp.headers["mcp-session-id"] ?? "");
+    expect(sid.length).toBeGreaterThan(5);
+
+    // Follow-up on active session + unknown session 404 + GET /mcp without session 400
+    const sessionCall = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}`,
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Accept: "application/json, text/event-stream",
+        "Mcp-Session-Id": sid,
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 2, method: "tools/list" }),
+    });
+    expect(sessionCall.status).toBe(200);
+
+    const missingSession = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}`,
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        "Mcp-Session-Id": "nonexistent-session",
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 3, method: "ping" }),
+    });
+    expect(missingSession.status).toBe(404);
+
+    const getNoSession = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/mcp",
+      method: "GET",
+      headers: { Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}` },
+    });
+    expect(getNoSession.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const badHost = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/health",
+      headers: { Host: "localhost:3000" },
+    });
+    expect(badHost.status).toBe(403);
+
+    const missingSid = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/messages",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    expect(missingSid.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const unknownSid = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/messages?sessionId=unknown",
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    expect(unknownSid.status).toBe(404);
+
+    // Connect over SSE endpoint and verify session endpoint event is received
+    const sseEndpoint = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
+      const req = http.get(
+        {
+          host: "127.0.0.1",
+          port: running.port,
+          path: "/sse",
+          headers: { Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}` },
+        },
+        (res) => {
+          let buf = "";
+          res.on("data", (chunk) => {
+            buf += Buffer.from(chunk).toString("utf8");
+            const match = /data:\s*(\/messages\?sessionId=[^\r\n]+)/.exec(buf);
+            if (match) {
+              req.destroy();
+              resolve(match[1].trim());
+            }
+          });
+        }
+      );
+      req.on("error", reject);
+    });
+    expect(sseEndpoint).toContain("/messages?sessionId=");
+  });
+
+  it("enforces fail-closed MCP_API_KEY outside loopback and strict Host/Origin syntax", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(16, 60_000, 0);
+    caches.push(cache);
+
+    expect(() =>
+      createHttpApp({
+        apiConfig: API_CONFIG,
+        serverConfig: { ...BASE_SERVER_CONFIG, httpHost: "0.0.0.0" },
+        cache,
+      })
+    ).toThrow(/non-loopback host/);
+
+    const created = createHttpApp({
+      apiConfig: API_CONFIG,
+      serverConfig: { ...BASE_SERVER_CONFIG, httpHost: "0.0.0.0" },
+      cache,
+      apiKey: "non-loopback-secret",
+    });
+    await created.cleanup();
+
+    const running = await startHttpServer({
+      apiConfig: API_CONFIG,
+      serverConfig: BASE_SERVER_CONFIG,
+      cache,
+      apiKey: "sec-key-2",
+    });
+    servers.push(running);
+
+    const badHostSyntax = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/health",
+      headers: { Host: "localhost:notaport" },
+    });
+    expect(badHostSyntax.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const credOrigin = `${"http://"}demo-user${":"}demo-token@localhost:5173`;
+    const badCredOrigin = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/health",
+      headers: {
+        Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}`,
+        Origin: credOrigin,
+      },
+    });
+    expect(badCredOrigin.status).toBe(403);
+
+    const badPathOrigin = await rawHttpRequest({
+      port: running.port,
+      path: "/health",
+      headers: {
+        Host: `127.0.0.1:${running.port}`,
+        Origin: "http://localhost:5173/evil",
+      },
+    });
+    expect(badPathOrigin.status).toBe(403);
+  });
+});

+import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import os from "node:os";
+import path from "node:path";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  DEFAULT_TESOURO_CKAN_URL,
+  DEFAULT_TESOURO_ORDS_URL,
+  DEFAULT_TRANSFEREGOV_URL,
+  isAllowedTesouroCkanUrl,
+  isAllowedTesouroOrdsUrl,
+  isAllowedTransferegovUrl,
+  loadServerConfiguration,
+  resolveApiConfig,
+  validateUrl,
+} from "../../lib/config/index.js";
+import { getOrdsRequestTimeoutMs, parsePositiveTimeoutMs } from "../../lib/config/timeouts.js";
+
+describe("Configuration & SSRF Allowlist Guards", () => {
+  const originalEnv = { ...process.env };
+  const tempDirs: string[] = [];
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    process.env = { ...originalEnv };
+    while (tempDirs.length > 0) {
+      const dir = tempDirs.pop()!;
+      rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("resolves default upstream URLs cleanly and appends trailing slashes", () => {
+    delete process.env.TESOURO_ORDS_BASE_URL;
+    delete process.env.TRANSFEREGOV_BASE_URL;
+    delete process.env.TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL;
+
+    const cfg = resolveApiConfig();
+    expect(cfg.tesouroOrdsBaseUrl).toBe(DEFAULT_TESOURO_ORDS_URL);
+    expect(cfg.transferegovBaseUrl).toBe(DEFAULT_TRANSFEREGOV_URL);
+    expect(cfg.tesouroCkanBaseUrl).toBe(DEFAULT_TESOURO_CKAN_URL);
+
+    expect(isAllowedTesouroOrdsUrl("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedTransferegovUrl("https://api.transferegov.gestao.gov.br")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedTesouroCkanUrl("https://tesourotransparente.gov.br/ckan/api/3/action")).toBe(
+      true
+    );
+  });
+
+  it("blocks private IPs, metadata hosts, plain HTTP, disallowed domains, and embedded credentials", () => {
+    const credPrefix = ["usr", "pwd"].join(":");
+    const blocked = [
+      "http://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/",
+      `https://${credPrefix}@apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/`,
+      "http://localhost:8080/ords/",
+      "https://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/",
+      "https://10.0.0.1/ords/",
+      "https://192.168.1.50/ords/",
+      "https://172.16.0.5/ords/",
+      "https://metadata.google.internal/computeMetadata/v1/",
+      "https://internal.local/api/",
+      "https://fc00::1/api/",
+      "https://evil.example.com/ords/",
+      "not-a-url",
+      "",
+    ];
+
+    for (const candidate of blocked) {
+      expect(
+        () => validateUrl(candidate, { allowedHosts: ["apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br"], env: "production" }),
+        `Expected production URL to be blocked: ${candidate}`
+      ).toThrow();
+    }
+
+    // Embedded credentials remain blocked even in development/test
+    expect(() =>
+      validateUrl(`http://${credPrefix}@localhost:9000/ords/`, { env: "test", allowLoopback: true })
+    ).toThrow(/credentials/i);
+  });
+
+  it("permits HTTP loopback only in development/test or when allowLoopback is true", () => {
+    const localUrl = validateUrl("http://127.0.0.1:4567/ords", { env: "test" });
+    expect(localUrl).toBe("http://127.0.0.1:4567/ords/");
+
+    expect(() => validateUrl("http://[::1]:4567/ords", { env: "production" })).toThrow();
+    expect(validateUrl("http://localhost:4567/ords", { allowLoopback: true, env: "production" })).toBe(
+      "http://localhost:4567/ords/"
+    );
+  });
+
+  it("loads .mcprc.json, applies env overrides, and validates resolved server config", () => {
+    const dir = mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "mcp-orcamento-cfg-"));
+    tempDirs.push(dir);
+    const rcPath = path.join(dir, ".mcprc.json");
+
+    writeFileSync(
+      rcPath,
+      JSON.stringify({
+        apiUrls: {
+          tesouroOrds: "http://127.0.0.1:9100/ords/",
+          transferegov: "http://127.0.0.1:9200/",
+          tesouroCkan: "http://127.0.0.1:9300/ckan/",
+        },
+        auth: {
+          headers: { "x-file-header": "from-file" },
+        },
+      }),
+      "utf8"
+    );
+
+    process.env.MCPRC_PATH = rcPath;
+    process.env.API_KEY_HEADER = "x-env-key";
+    process.env.API_KEY_VALUE = "env-val";
+    process.env.TESOURO_CKAN_BASE_URL = "https://www.tesourotransparente.gov.br/ckan/api/3/action/";
+    process.env.MCP_HTTP_PORT = "4321";
+    process.env.MCP_TRANSPORT = "HTTP";
+    process.env.MCP_HTTP_HOST = "127.0.0.1";
+
+    const loaded = loadServerConfiguration({ env: "test" });
+    expect(loaded.apiConfig.tesouroOrdsBaseUrl).toBe("http://127.0.0.1:9100/ords/");
+    expect(loaded.apiConfig.transferegovBaseUrl).toBe("http://127.0.0.1:9200/");
+    expect(loaded.apiConfig.tesouroCkanBaseUrl).toBe(
+      "https://www.tesourotransparente.gov.br/ckan/api/3/action/"
+    );
+    expect(loaded.apiConfig.authHeaders).toEqual({
+      "x-file-header": "from-file",
+      "x-env-key": "env-val",
+    });
+    expect(loaded.serverConfig.transport).toBe("http");
+    expect(loaded.serverConfig.httpPort).toBe(4321);
+    expect(loaded.serverConfig.httpHost).toBe("127.0.0.1");
+
+    // Malformed .mcprc.json throws ConfigurationError
+    writeFileSync(rcPath, "{broken json", "utf8");
+    expect(() => resolveApiConfig({ configFilePath: rcPath })).toThrow(/Failed to parse config file/);
+  });
+
+  it("parses ORDS and positive integer timeout overrides safely", () => {
+    expect(parsePositiveTimeoutMs("25000", 15000)).toBe(25000);
+    expect(parsePositiveTimeoutMs("0", 15000)).toBe(15000);
+    expect(parsePositiveTimeoutMs("abc", 15000)).toBe(15000);
+    expect(getOrdsRequestTimeoutMs("20000")).toBe(20000);
+    expect(getOrdsRequestTimeoutMs("invalid")).toBe(15000);
+  });
+});

+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import {
+  getCircuitBreakerForOrigin,
+  getCircuitBreakerMetrics,
+  httpJson,
+  resetCircuitBreakers,
+} from "../../lib/core/http-client.js";
+import {
+  CircuitBreaker,
+  CircuitBreakerOpenError,
+} from "../../lib/infrastructure/http/circuit-breaker.js";
+import {
+  createTimeoutController,
+  isAbortOrTimeoutError,
+  raceWithTimeoutAndSignal,
+} from "../../lib/shared/utils/timeout.js";
+
+describe("HTTP Client & Circuit Breaker", () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    resetCircuitBreakers();
+  });
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+    resetCircuitBreakers();
+  });
+
+  it("opens CircuitBreaker on threshold failures and closes after HALF_OPEN recovery", async () => {
+    const breaker = new CircuitBreaker({
+      service: "https://api.test",
+      failureThreshold: 2,
+      resetTimeoutMs: 25,
+      halfOpenMaxAttempts: 2,
+    });
+
+    expect(breaker.getState()).toBe("CLOSED");
+
+    await expect(
+      breaker.execute(async () => {
+        throw new Error("boom-1");
+      })
+    ).rejects.toThrow("boom-1");
+
+    await expect(
+      breaker.execute(async () => {
+        throw new Error("boom-2");
+      })
+    ).rejects.toThrow("boom-2");
+
+    expect(breaker.getState()).toBe("OPEN");
+    await expect(breaker.execute(async () => "blocked")).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
+      CircuitBreakerOpenError
+    );
+
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 35));
+    expect(breaker.getState()).toBe("HALF_OPEN");
+
+    expect(await breaker.execute(async () => "ok-1")).toBe("ok-1");
+    expect(await breaker.execute(async () => "ok-2")).toBe("ok-2");
+    expect(breaker.getState()).toBe("CLOSED");
+    expect(breaker.getMetrics().successes).toBe(2);
+  });
+
+  it("retries transient 5xx responses, ignores 404 for circuit breaking, and parses malformed JSON safely", async () => {
+    const fetchMock = vi
+      .fn()
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(new Response("err", { status: 503, statusText: "Unavailable" }))
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(
+        new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), { status: 200 })
+      );
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchMock);
+
+    const recovered = await httpJson("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/test", {
+      retries: 1,
+      baseDelayMs: 0,
+      requestId: "req-1",
+    });
+    expect(recovered.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(recovered.data).toEqual({ ok: true });
+    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
+
+    // 404 is non-retryable and does not open the circuit breaker
+    const fetch404 = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Response("nope", { status: 404 }));
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetch404);
+    const breaker = getCircuitBreakerForOrigin("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br");
+    const r404 = await httpJson("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/missing", {
+      retries: 2,
+      baseDelayMs: 0,
+      circuitBreaker: breaker,
+    });
+    expect(r404.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(r404.error).toContain("HTTP 404");
+    expect(fetch404).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(breaker.getState()).toBe("CLOSED");
+    expect(getCircuitBreakerMetrics().length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
+
+    // Invalid JSON from 200 response returns structured error
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Response("not-json{", { status: 200 })));
+    const badJson = await httpJson("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/badjson", {
+      retries: 0,
+    });
+    expect(badJson.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(badJson.error).toContain("Invalid JSON");
+  });
+
+  it("handles pre-aborted signals and timeout utilities cleanly", async () => {
+    const parent = new AbortController();
+    parent.abort();
+
+    const aborted = await httpJson("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/test", {
+      signal: parent.signal,
+    });
+    expect(aborted.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(aborted.error).toContain("aborted");
+
+    const linked = createTimeoutController(500);
+    expect(linked.didTimeout()).toBe(false);
+    expect(linked.wasAbortedByParent()).toBe(false);
+    linked.cleanup();
+
+    expect(isAbortOrTimeoutError({ name: "AbortError" })).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAbortOrTimeoutError({ name: "TimeoutError" })).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAbortOrTimeoutError(new Error("other"))).toBe(false);
+
+    await expect(
+      raceWithTimeoutAndSignal(new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100)), 15, undefined, "SlowOp")
+    ).rejects.toThrow(/timed out/);
+
+    await expect(raceWithTimeoutAndSignal(Promise.resolve("fast"), 200)).resolves.toBe("fast");
+
+    const preAborted = new AbortController();
+    preAborted.abort();
+    const ctrlAborted = createTimeoutController(100, preAborted.signal);
+    expect(ctrlAborted.wasAbortedByParent()).toBe(true);
+    await expect(
+      raceWithTimeoutAndSignal(new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50)), 100, preAborted.signal)
+    ).rejects.toThrow(/aborted/);
+
+    const midAbort = new AbortController();
+    const ctrlMid = createTimeoutController(500, midAbort.signal);
+    midAbort.abort();
+    expect(ctrlMid.wasAbortedByParent()).toBe(true);
+    ctrlMid.cleanup();
+
+    // Open circuit breaker returns structured { ok: false } in httpJson
+    const openBreaker = new CircuitBreaker({ failureThreshold: 1, resetTimeoutMs: 5000 });
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error("ECONNREFUSED")));
+    await httpJson("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/fail", {
+      retries: 0,
+      circuitBreaker: openBreaker,
+    });
+    const blocked = await httpJson("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/fail", {
+      retries: 0,
+      circuitBreaker: openBreaker,
+    });
+    expect(blocked.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(blocked.error).toContain("OPEN");
+
+    // Empty 200 response -> ok: true, data: null; invalid URL origin falls back cleanly
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Response("", { status: 200 })));
+    const emptyBody = await httpJson("not-a-valid-url", { retries: 0 });
+    expect(emptyBody.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(emptyBody.data).toBeNull();
+
+    // Timeout during pending fetch triggers AbortController and retries cleanly
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn().mockImplementation(
+        (_url: string, init?: RequestInit) =>
+          new Promise((_resolve, reject) => {
+            init?.signal?.addEventListener("abort", () => {
+              const err = new Error("The operation was aborted");
+              err.name = "AbortError";
+              reject(err);
+            });
+          })
+      )
+    );
+    const timedOut = await httpJson("https://apidatalake.tesouro.gov.br/ords/slow", {
+      timeout: 10,
+      retries: 1,
+      baseDelayMs: 1,
+    });
+    expect(timedOut.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(timedOut.error).toContain("timed out");
+  });
+});

+import { Client } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js";
+import { InMemoryTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/inMemory.js";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../../lib/core/cache.js";
+import { createMCPServer } from "../../lib/core/mcp-server.js";
+
+describe("MCP Server Handlers (Stdio / Protocol Layer)", () => {
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+  });
+
+  it("handles listTools (8), empty listResources, readResource rejection, 101-call rate limiting, and bounded output", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(32, 60_000, 0);
+    const server = createMCPServer({
+      cache,
+      maxToolOutputChars: 1500,
+    });
+    const client = new Client(
+      { name: "vitest-mcp-client", version: "1.0.0" },
+      { capabilities: {} }
+    );
+
+    const [clientTransport, serverTransport] = InMemoryTransport.createLinkedPair();
+    const stderrSpy = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
+
+    await Promise.all([server.connect(serverTransport), client.connect(clientTransport)]);
+
+    const toolsRes = await client.listTools();
+    expect(toolsRes.tools).toHaveLength(8);
+
+    const resourcesRes = await client.listResources();
+    expect(resourcesRes.resources).toEqual([]);
+
+    await expect(client.readResource({ uri: "orcamento://invalid" })).rejects.toThrow(
+      /Unknown resource URI/
+    );
+
+    // 100 invalid offline calls hit Zod validation without fetch; 101st hits session rate limit
+    for (let i = 1; i <= 101; i++) {
+      const res = await client.callTool({ name: "siconfi_entes", arguments: {} });
+      expect(res.isError, `Call ${i} should set isError: true`).toBe(true);
+      const firstText = (res.content as Array<{ type: string; text: string }>)[0]?.text ?? "";
+      if (i === 101) {
+        expect(firstText).toContain("Rate limit exceeded");
+      }
+    }
+
+    await client.close();
+    await server.close();
+    expect(stderrSpy.mock.calls.some((args) => String(args[0]).includes("[metrics]"))).toBe(true);
+    cache.destroy();
+  });
+
+  it("bounds oversized tool JSON payloads cleanly using serializeBoundedToolOutput", async () => {
+    const hugeItems = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({
+      cod_ibge: String(1000000 + i),
+      ente: `Município Teste Longo ${i} `.repeat(6),
+      capital: "0 ",
+      uf: "SP",
+      esfera: "M",
+      cnpj: "00000000000100",
+    }));
+
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(
+        new Response(JSON.stringify({ items: hugeItems }), {
+          status: 200,
+          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        })
+      )
+    );
+
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(16, 60_000, 0);
+    const server = createMCPServer({
+      cache,
+      maxToolOutputChars: 1200,
+    });
+    const client = new Client({ name: "bounded-client", version: "1.0.0" }, { capabilities: {} });
+    const [clientTransport, serverTransport] = InMemoryTransport.createLinkedPair();
+    vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
+
+    await Promise.all([server.connect(serverTransport), client.connect(clientTransport)]);
+
+    const callRes = await client.callTool({
+      name: "siconfi_entes",
+      arguments: { uf: "SP", limit: 40 },
+    });
+    expect(callRes.isError).toBe(false);
+    const text = (callRes.content as Array<{ text: string }>)[0].text;
+    expect(text.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1200);
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(text) as { truncated?: boolean };
+    expect(parsed.truncated).toBe(true);
+
+    await client.close();
+    await server.close();
+    cache.destroy();
+  });
+});
